### [75] [Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) for aerodynamic prediction: a comparison with MLPs and GNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27126)
*Miguel Jaraiz,Fermin Gutierrez,Pablo Yeste,Miguel Sánchez-Domínguez,Eusebio Valero,Gonzalo Rubio,Lucas Lacasa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了KAN在流体动力学代理建模中的性能，发现其与MLP性能相当（略逊），最优模型为GNN但训练更耗时；KAN虽复杂度低、训练快，但存在训练不稳定问题且高度依赖超参数优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关于KAN是否优于MLP存在争议，本文旨在评估KAN在流体动力学代理建模（具体为翼型表面压力分布预测）中的表现，并与MLP和GNN进行对比。

Method: 以亚音速和跨音速翼型的表面压力系数预测为任务，对比KAN、MLP和GNN三种模型在性能、复杂度、训练速度及稳定性方面的表现。

Result: KAN能较好地预测压力系数并在马赫数和攻角间插值，但性能略逊于MLP；GNN性能最佳但训练时间最长。KAN模型复杂度最低、训练最快，但存在训练不稳定问题且对超参数高度敏感。

Conclusion: 在翼型压力分布预测任务中，KAN性能与MLP相当但略差，未表现出优于传统架构的显著优势；GNN虽性能最优但训练成本高。KAN的实际应用受限于训练不稳定性和超参数敏感性。

Abstract: Kolmogorov Arnold networks (KAN) have recently been introduced as a (deep) neural network architecture whose trainable parameters adapt the activation functions, instead of the coefficients of the affine transformations at the core of traditional architectures such as deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs). This architecture builds on the Kolmogorov-Arnold theorem, which endows it with universal approximation properties. While the advent of KANs has been received with excitement, there is a current debate about the possible KAN supremacy over deep multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) for classic fields such as symbolic regression, generic-purpose machine learning, natural language processing or computer vision. Here we assess the performance of KANs --and its nuanced comparison against MLPs and graph neural networks (GNNs)-- in the realm of fluid dynamics surrogate modelling. To that aim, we consider the task of predicting the surface pressure distribution over subsonic and transonic airfoils, a canonical task in aerodynamics. Our results show that KAN models show good performance in predicting the whole pressure coefficients and is able to interpolate across Mach numbers and angles of attack, however its performance is comparable --marginally inferior-- to a suitably trained MLP, where best performance is achieved by a GNN at the expense or requiring lengthier training. While the optimal KAN model have typically much lower complexity than MLP and GNN --hence resulting in faster training--, we find that KANs suffer from training instabilities, and their performance is highly dependent on a proper hyperparameter optimisation.

</details>


### [76] [fTNN: a tensor neural network for fractional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27140)
*Qingkui Ma,Hehu Xie,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出fTNN——一种确定性的张量神经网络子空间方法，用于求解有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题（分数阶泊松方程和时变分数阶对流扩散方程）。通过几何自适应积分分裂技术将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内远场和分析外远场三部分，结合Gauss-Jacobi和Gauss求积实现完全确定性积分框架。构造边界奇异性感知的试验函数，并提出自动选择主导指数和评估损失函数的策略。对于时变问题，设计时空可分离神经网络并结合交替子空间优化策略。数值实验表明，该方法在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于现有fPINN和蒙特卡洛方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数阶神经网络方法（如fPINN）和蒙特卡洛方法在处理有界域上的分数阶拉普拉斯问题时面临精度不足、计算成本高、难以处理强边界奇异性和长时间模拟等挑战，亟需一种高精度、确定性的数值方法。

Method: 1) 几何自适应积分分裂：根据空间依赖的近场半径将分数阶拉普拉斯算子分解为奇异近场、规则内远场和分析外远场三项；2) 完全确定性求积：近场奇异径向积分用Gauss-Jacobi求积，规则径向积分用Gauss求积，角度变量用确定性角度求积；3) 边界奇异性感知的试验函数：利用显式边界特征增强的基函数，并设计两种策略自动选择主导指数并评估损失函数；4) 时空可分离神经网络：将时空残差分解为低维时间积分和空间积分之和，结合交替神经网络子空间优化策略进行高效训练。

Result: 在测试基准上获得高精度，在强边界奇异性和长时间模拟问题上显著优于fPINN和蒙特卡洛基线方法。

Conclusion: fTNN作为确定性张量神经网络子空间方法，为分数阶拉普拉斯问题提供了高精度、高效的求解框架，尤其在处理边界奇异性和长时间演化方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: We develop the fTNN, a deterministic tensor neural network subspace method for problems involving the fractional Laplacian on bounded domains, taking the fractional Poisson equation and time-dependent fractional advection-diffusion equation as typical representatives. The work employs a geometry-adapted integration split featuring a spatially dependent near-field radius, which decomposes the fractional Laplacian into three contributions: a singular near field, a regular interior far field, and an analytical exterior far field. Then the singular radial integrals are treated by Gauss-Jacobi quadrature, the regular radial integrals by Gauss quadrature, and the angular variables by deterministic angular quadrature, yielding a fully deterministic integration framework of the fractional Laplacian operator. To accurately resolve low-regularity solutions and the associated loss functional, we construct boundary-singularity-aware trial functions enriched with explicit boundary features, and propose two strategies for automatically selecting the leading exponent and evaluating the loss function from the singularity structure induced by the fractional operator, or jointly by the fractional operator and the source term. For time-dependent fractional PDEs, we design a spatiotemporally separable neural network that factorizes the time-space residual into a sum of low-dimensional temporal and spatial integrals, and we integrate this representation with an alternating neural network subspace optimization strategy for efficient training. Numerical experiments show that the proposed framework attains high accuracy on the tested benchmarks and improves substantially over existing fPINN and Monte Carlo baselines, particularly for problems with strong boundary singularities and long-time simulations.

</details>


### [77] [Stochastic Gradient Optimization with Model-Assisted Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27171)
*Jonne Pohjankukka,Jukka Heikkonen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于模型辅助抽样的梯度估计框架，将小批量梯度下降问题与调查抽样理论结合，利用辅助梯度预测模型构建更高效的梯度估计器，在71-86%的实验中获得性能提升，尤其在使用AdamW等动量优化器时，仅需约一半训练轮次即可达到更好的泛化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中的小批量随机梯度下降存在梯度噪声问题，现有方差缩减技术（如SVRG、SAG）和自适应优化器虽可缓解但可能引入额外计算开销，本文旨在通过引入调查抽样理论来桥接机器学习优化与抽样估计，构建更高效且易集成的梯度估计方法。

Method: 将数据集视为固定有限总体，梯度视为基于样本的估计量，结合辅助梯度预测模型构建模型辅助抽样框架，使均匀抽样成为无辅助信息时的特例；该方法可与现有优化器无缝集成。

Result: 在合成数据和六个基准数据集上，71-86%的实验获得性能提升；在中维输入空间上表现尤为突出；使用AdamW等动量优化器时，约一半训练轮次即可获得更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 所提模型辅助抽样框架有效降低了梯度估计方差，与现有优化器兼容性强，在不改变优化器动态特性的前提下提升了训练效率和泛化能力。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of variance in stochastic gradient estimation for machine learning optimization. Deep learning relies on mini-batch methods such as stochastic gradient descent, which approximate full gradients but introduce noise, creating trade-offs between convergence stability, speed, and generalization. Existing methods, including variance reduction techniques (e.g., SVRG and SAG) and adaptive optimizers, aim to mitigate gradient noise but may introduce additional computational overhead. We propose a model-assisted sampling framework that interprets mini-batch gradients through survey sampling theory, treating the dataset as a fixed finite population and gradients as sample-based estimates. Our aim is to bridge machine learning optimization and survey sampling theory by combining their perspectives on sample-based estimation and variance reduction. By incorporating auxiliary gradient-prediction models, we construct more efficient gradient estimators, with uniform sampling arising as a special case when no auxiliary information is used. Our approach integrates easily with existing optimizers, improving efficiency without altering their dynamics. Empirical results on synthetic and six benchmark datasets show performance gains in 71-86% of the experiments, particularly for medium-sized input spaces in our benchmarks. Notably, with momentum-based optimizers such as AdamW, the proposed estimator achieves clearly better generalization in roughly half the training epochs compared to baseline estimator.

</details>


### [78] [RecallRisk-BERT: A Multi-Task Framework for Post-Report Medical Device Recall Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27174)
*Ali Semih Atalay,Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，利用54,165条FDA医疗器械召回记录，联合预测召回严重等级（Class I/II/III）和根因类别（9类），通过PubMedBERT文本表示与结构化特征嵌入融合，在严重性预测中达到准确率0.963、宏F1 0.856、ROC-AUC 0.974，根因风险排序与观察模式高度一致（rho=0.983）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将召回发生预测与根因分析分开处理，缺乏对召回严重等级和根因类别的联合建模，无法支持报告后召回分诊、严重性评估和根因解读的综合需求。

Method: 构建包含54,165条2002-2025年FDA医疗器械召回记录的数据集；分别评估经典机器学习与基于Boosting的模型；开发RecallRisk-BERT多任务模型，融合PubMedBERT文本表示与产品代码、法规编号、医学专业等结构化分类特征的嵌入表示，同时预测召回严重等级（3类）和根因类别（9类）。

Result: 单任务严重性预测中，基于LightGBM的文本-表格配置最优（准确率0.963，宏F1 0.856，ROC-AUC 0.974）；多任务设置下RecallRisk-BERT显著优于单任务PubMedBERT基线；模型风险排序与根因严重性模式高度一致（rho=0.983, p=1.936e-6）。

Conclusion: 文本-表格联合学习可支持可扩展的报告后召回分诊、监管决策辅助和基于模型的根因风险分析。

Abstract: Medical device recalls are a critical regulatory mechanism for protecting patient safety. The growing volume of FDA recall records presents challenges in post-report recall triage, severity assessment, and root-cause interpretation. Existing studies mostly address recall occurrence prediction or root-cause analysis separately, while joint modeling of recall severity and root-cause categories has received limited attention. We develop an automated recall triage framework using 54,165 FDA medical device recall records from openFDA, covering the period from 2002 to October 2025. We first evaluate classical machine learning and boosting-based models for recall severity and root-cause category prediction. We then develop RecallRisk-BERT, a multi-task model that combines PubMedBERT-based textual representations of recall narratives with embedding-based representations of structured categorical features, including product code, regulation number, and medical specialty. The model simultaneously predicts recall severity (Class I/II/III) and a consolidated root-cause category (9 classes). Performance was evaluated using accuracy, macro-averaged precision, recall, F1-score, and ROC-AUC. In single-task severity prediction, our LightGBM-based text--tabular configuration achieved the strongest performance, with an accuracy of 0.963, macro-F1 of 0.856, and ROC-AUC of 0.974. In the multi-task setting, RecallRisk-BERT substantially outperformed the single-task PubMedBERT baseline. Model-derived risk rankings were strongly consistent with observed root-cause severity patterns (rho = 0.983, p = 1.936e-6). These findings indicate that text--tabular learning can support scalable post-report recall triage, regulatory decision support, and model-based root-cause risk analysis.

</details>


### [79] [Automating Potential-based Reward Shaping with Vision Language Model Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27180)
*Henrik Müller,Daniel Kudenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-PBRS框架，利用轻量级视觉语言模型（VLM）的偏好反馈学习势能函数，实现基于势能的奖励塑形（PBRS），在保持最优策略集不变的前提下提升稀疏奖励环境下的样本效率，并抵御奖励黑客攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励缺乏中间反馈，难以引导探索和正确归因；朴素奖励塑形易导致奖励黑客攻击；传统PBRS需要专家设计的势能函数，成本高昂。

Method: 使用轻量级VLM对图像对进行偏好比较，基于这些偏好标签训练势能函数模型，再将其集成到PBRS框架中进行奖励塑形，保持最优策略集不变。

Result: 在Meta-World和Franka Kitchen环境中验证，该方法提升了样本效率，对奖励黑客攻击具有鲁棒性，且偏好标签准确率与样本效率改进之间存在正相关关系。

Conclusion: 首次将VLM偏好学习用于合成PBRS势能函数，利用小型VLM实现了低成本、原则性的解决方案，实验证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Sparse rewards are inherently challenging for reinforcement learning agents as they lack intermediate feedback to guide exploration and to correctly attribute the sparse success rewards to relevant parts of the trajectory. Naive reward shaping can induce reward hacking, yielding policies that exploit auxiliary signals instead of solving the intended task. Potential-based reward shaping (PBRS) guarantees preservation of the optimal policy set, but requires the definition of a heuristic potential function over the state space. In this work, we introduce the VLM-guided PBRS framework VLM-PBRS that learns the potential function directly from vision language model (VLM) feedback. We query a lightweight VLM to obtain preferences over image pairs and train a model of the potential function using these preferences. As this approach is based on potential-based reward shaping, it preserves the original optimal policies, and removes the need for expert-designed reward shaping terms. Because large VLMs are prohibitively expensive to invoke repeatedly during policy learning, we employ smaller, more computationally efficient VLMs. Although the resulting preference labels are less accurate, empirical evidence shows that the preference labels can still be used to accelerate learning. We validate our method empirically in the Meta-World and Franka Kitchen environments and highlight the connection between VLM preference label accuracy and sample efficiency improvements. Our contributions are threefold: (1) the first application of VLM preference-based learning to synthesize a potential function for PBRS, (2) a principled, low-cost solution that leverages small VLMs, and (3) extensive empirical demonstration of improved sample efficiency and robustness to reward hacking.

</details>


### [80] [Explaining Temporal Graph Neural Networks via Feature-induced Information Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27201)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于归一化相关度量（NRM）框架的归因方法，通过分析事件时序图神经网络（ETGNN）中所有事件相关变量的完整信息流，实现对ETGNN的全局可解释性，在合成和真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETGNN解释方法仅关注部分信息流（从事件相关嵌入到输出），忽略了通过事件诱导变量的重要路径，而这些变量在捕获长时序依赖中起到关键作用。

Method: 基于NRM框架，量化来自事件嵌入和通过事件诱导变量的完整信息流；引入模块化解构程序以处理ETGNN的复杂架构；确保层间潜变量的可比性并支持事件间高阶交互分析。

Result: 在流行病追踪、社会动力学合成数据集以及政治事件网络真实数据集上，定性和定量实验均表明该方法在解释性能和人类可解释性上持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过覆盖事件诱导变量的完整信息流，所提方法弥补了ETGNN可解释性的关键空白，为复杂时序图神经网络提供了更全面、可比较且支持高阶分析的归因解释框架。

Abstract: Event-based Temporal Graph Neural Networks (ETGNNs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of applications, including social network analysis, epidemic tracing, recommender systems, and political event forecasting. However, their increasing complexity poses significant challenges for explainability. Existing explanation methods focus only on a subset of the information flow within ETGNNs, typically tracing contributions from the event-related embeddings to the output. Consequently, they overlook the important pathways through event-induced variables, which mediate interactions between nodes and thereby play a central role in capturing long-range temporal dependencies. To overcome this limitation, we propose a novel attribution method that analyzes the \emph{entire} information flow through all event-associated variables. Our method is built upon the recent Normalized Relevance Measure (NRM) framework, which enables explicit quantification of information flow originating from event embeddings as well as information flow passing through event-induced variables. It also ensures comparability of latent variables across layers, and supports higher-order analysis of interactions between events. To handle the architectural complexity of ETGNNs, we extend the NRM framework with a modular decomposition procedure that facilitates the systematic construction of relevance structure for complex neural architectures. We evaluate our approach on two synthetic datasets for epidemic tracing and social dynamics, as well as a real-world dataset of political event networks. Our qualitative and quantitative experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing explanation approaches while producing more human-interpretable explanations.

</details>


### [81] [Graph Neural Networks Applications Across Domains: All Insights You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27202)
*Abderaouf Bahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统梳理了图神经网络的设计空间、谱域与空域方法的统一原理、Weisfeiler-Leman层次与表达能力的关系，并在12个应用领域中分析了图结构的成本效益、架构选择与实际部署中的关键约束（过度平滑、过度挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性、可解释性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答一个关键问题：图结构在哪些场景下值得其计算成本，哪些场景下不值得。图神经网络已成为处理关系数据的默认模型，但需要厘清其实际收益与成本。

Method: 围绕单一设计空间组织领域，从共享第一原理推导谱域和空域公式，将表达能力与Weisfeiler-Leman层次连接，并在12个应用领域中进行跨领域对比分析。

Result: 揭示了多个跨领域模式：异质性和规模几乎在所有领域削弱相同模型，时序图始终比静态图更难，公共排行榜上的架构很少被实际部署。指出了许多报告增益来源于弱基线或有利的数据划分。

Conclusion: 过度平滑、过度挤压、鲁棒性、分布偏移、公平性、可解释性不是封闭的检查清单，而是决定模型能否被采用的实际约束条件。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have moved from a niche representation-learning technique to the default model class wherever data carry relational structure. The interesting question is no longer whether message passing helps on a given dataset, but where graph structure earns its computational cost and where it does not. This survey organises the field around a single design space, derives the spectral and spatial formulations from shared first principles, and connects expressive power to the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy with explicit statements of what current architectures can and cannot separate. Against that methodological backbone we examine twelve application domains, among them recommendation and social networks, knowledge graphs and language-model integration, drug discovery and molecular property learning, healthcare and neuroscience, computer vision, traffic and urban computing, power and renewable-energy systems, wireless and sixth-generation networks, fraud and cybersecurity, industrial prognostics, materials science, and climate modelling. For each domain we specify the graph-construction choices and their costs, identify which architecture families dominate and why, and separate reported gains from artefacts of weak baselines or favourable splits. A cross-domain comparison exposes recurring patterns: heterophily and scale undercut the same models almost everywhere, temporal graphs remain harder than their static counterparts, and the architectures that top public leaderboards are seldom the ones that reach deployment. We treat over-smoothing, over-squashing, robustness, distribution shift, fairness, and explainability not as a closing checklist but as the constraints that decide adoption.

</details>


### [82] [The Geometry of Updates: Fisher Alignment at Vocabulary Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27242)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对共享词汇表的LLM家族（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组序列）中无监督源选择问题，提出FisherSketch方法：通过Fisher对齐的核均值嵌入余弦分解，仅需一次流式扫描即可计算任务间头（head）的Fisher对齐度，避免传统几何度量的计算负担，且当表示相似度（如CKA）无法区分任务时仍保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学序列领域（如SMILES、蛋白质、基因组），候选语料库共享分词器但预测目标不同，导致表示相似度指标（如CKA）在无标签误差几何假设下无法有效指导迁移源选择，而经典更新几何度量的计算复杂度在词汇表规模下不可行。

Method: 证明在共享输出头设定下，头Fisher对齐等价于联合激活-误差空间中核均值嵌入的余弦值，从而分解为激活因子、误差因子和耦合因子。FisherSketch通过单次流式扫描直接估计该余弦值，生成16KB的任务签名（m=4096）和192KB的流式状态，避免显式构造Fisher矩阵。

Result: K=128,256的头Fisher对齐在流式扫描下可行，任务签名足够小可存储于模型哈希旁。在Llama-3.1-8B的verbalizer偏移实验中，FisherSketch在激活相似度无法区分任务时仍保持信息量。共享参数和内部层验证进一步证明了有效性。

Conclusion: FisherSketch提供了一种轻量级、有信息量的源选择方案，同时其签名和边际量可作为诊断工具，用于研究LLM任务相似性是由激活、误差还是它们的耦合驱动。

Abstract: Training-free source selection for LLM families with shared vocabularies arises in scientific string domains such as SMILES, protein, and genomic sequences, where candidate corpora share a tokenizer but differ in prediction targets. This creates an activation-dark regime: representation-similarity metrics can be uninformative without assumptions about label-conditioned error geometry, while classical update-geometry metrics are computationally prohibitive at vocabulary scale. We show that, in a shared-output head setting, representation metrics (e.g., CKA) are non-identifiable for transfer; models can share identical representations yet have orthogonal head updates. The key identity is that head Fisher alignment is exactly a cosine between kernel mean embeddings in the joint activation-error space, exposing activation, error, and coupling factors rather than requiring a materialized Fisher matrix. FisherSketch estimates this cosine directly in a single streaming pass, making K=128,256 head Fisher alignment practical with a 16 KB task signature (m=4096) and a 192 KB per-task streaming state, small enough to store next to a model hash, but encoding transfer-relevant update structure. Beyond source selection, the same signatures and marginals provide a diagnostic instrument for studying whether LLM task similarity is driven by activations, errors, or their coupling; shared-parameter and internal-layer validations, together with Llama-3.1-8B verbalizer-shift experiments, show that FisherSketch remains informative when activation similarity cannot distinguish tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Effective Covariance Dynamics in Solvable High-Dimensional GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27246)
*Andrew Bond,Zafer Doğan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个可解的高维生成对抗网络（GAN）训练模型，其中线性生成器从具有结构化潜在协方差的数据中学习低维子空间。通过考虑类别相关、相关且非零均值的潜在结构，证明了微观随机训练过程在高维极限下收敛到由有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程，并揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可以提升弱方向超过可学习阈值，而过强的相关则破坏恢复稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的可解GAN分析假设无条件信号与对角潜在协方差，未考虑实际数据中类别相关、相关且非零均值的潜在结构。本文旨在扩展多特征判别器设置，纳入这类异质性，以更贴近真实场景。

Method: 采用二次能量判别器，将异质性通过概率加权的有效二阶矩进入动力学。在高维极限下，证明微观随机训练过程收敛到由有效协方差驱动的确定性常微分方程。在匹配协方差特化下进行稳定性分析，得到由学习率和噪声水平决定的模式可解区间。

Result: 揭示了信号增强机制：低秩相关可提升弱方向超过可学习阈值，而过强相关则破坏稳定性。数值模拟验证了ODE、相界和增强机制。在MNIST、FashionMNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，有信息的生成器协方差可改善与数据驱动参考子空间的对齐。

Conclusion: 本文扩展了可解GAN理论至更一般的潜在结构，揭示了相关性和非零均值对学习动力学的影响，为理解GAN中结构化协方差的作用提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a solvable high-dimensional model of generative adversarial network (GAN) training in which a linear generator learns a low-dimensional subspace from data with structured latent covariance. Prior solvable GAN analyses assume unconditional signals with diagonal latent covariance; we extend the multi-feature discriminator setting to class-dependent, correlated, and non-zero-mean latent structure. For the quadratic energy discriminator, all such heterogeneity enters the dynamics through a probability-weighted effective second moment. We prove that the stochastic microscopic training process converges, in the high-dimensional limit, to deterministic ordinary differential equations governed by this effective covariance. In the matched-covariance specialization, the stability analysis yields a mode-wise solvable interval determined by the learning rates and noise level: learning begins when the leading effective eigenvalue crosses the lower threshold, while full recovery requires all relevant effective modes to remain within the interval. This reveals a signal-boosting mechanism: low-rank correlations can lift weak directions above the learnability threshold, whereas overly strong correlations destabilize recovery. Numerical simulations validate the ODE, phase boundary, and boosting mechanism. Experiments on MNIST, FashionMNIST, and CIFAR-10 further show that informed generator covariance improves alignment with the data-driven reference subspace.

</details>


### [84] [RSPC: A Benchmark for Modeling Stress and Psychiatric Conditions in Digitally Mediated Relationships using Psychiatrist Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27247)
*Parmitha Vangapandu,Sai Ganesh Mokkapati,Sathwik Narkedimilli,MSVPJ Sathvik,Timothy Liu,Simon See,Johannes C. Eichstaedt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了关系压力与精神病理学语料库（RSPC），包含1,799条由精神科医生标注的Reddit帖子，涵盖情绪障碍（焦虑和抑郁）、关系压力触发因素及关系阶段信息。通过微调Transformer模型和大语言模型的基准测试，发现不同模型在疾病分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测任务上存在明显差异，Claude-3-Haiku在疾病分类上最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最强（Macro-F1=0.519）。研究还发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。RSPC为考虑关系背景的NLP任务建立了基准，推动了从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NLP心理健康研究通常将心理疾病建模为孤立现象，忽略了人际背景和社交情境对心理健康的影响。作者旨在通过引入关系背景（如异地恋）来捕捉心理健康困扰及其相关的关系触发因素，从而推动更全面的心理健康建模。

Method: 构建了RSPC语料库，包含1,799条关于异地恋的Reddit帖子，由精神科医生标注诊断类别（焦虑、抑郁）、关系压力触发因素和关系阶段。使用七种微调Transformer模型和五种大语言模型进行多标签疾病分类、关系触发检测和时间阶段预测三个任务的基准测试。

Result: Claude-3-Haiku在疾病分类上表现最佳（Macro-F1=0.538），GPT-4o在关系触发检测上最强（Macro-F1=0.519）。不同模型家族在不同任务上表现出明显的任务依赖性差异。发现焦虑障碍与慢性关系不确定性之间存在强关联。

Conclusion: RSPC为考虑关系背景的NLP任务建立了基准，支持从个体中心向情境感知的心理健康建模转变，能够更好地捕捉心理困扰的社会和时间动态。

Abstract: In NLP, mental health conditions are often modeled as isolated phenomena, without interpersonal context. We use Reddit posts about long-distance relationships to capture both mental health distress and associated relational triggers. We introduce the Relational Stress and Psychiatry Corpus (RSPC) containing 1,799 Reddit posts annotated by psychiatrists for diagnostic categories, including the most prevalent mood disorders (anxiety and depression), relational stressor triggers, and indications of relationship phase. We benchmark seven fine-tuned transformer models and five large language models across multi-label disorder classification, relational trigger detection, and temporal phase prediction tasks. We find clear task-dependent differences between model families, with Claude-3-Haiku achieving the best disorder classification performance (Macro-F1 = 0.538) and GPT-4o obtaining the strongest relational trigger detection performance (Macro-F1 = 0.519), suggesting distinct model capabilities. We further find strong associations between anxiety disorders and chronic relational uncertainty. Overall, RSPC establishes a benchmark for NLP tasks that consider relational context and supports a shift from individual-centric to context-aware mental health modeling that captures the social and temporal dynamics of distress.

</details>


### [85] [BetXplain: An Explanation-Annotated Dataset for Detecting Manipulative Betting Advertisements on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27274)
*MSVPJ Sathvik,Parmitha Vangapadu,Nishit Rane,Sathwik Narkedimilli,Mark Lee,Akrati Saxena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个来自Instagram和Reddit的博彩广告数据集，该数据集包含人工标注的操作性欺骗性广告及其解释，并分析了博彩广告中常用的说服策略及其对用户心理健康的影响。该框架可支持浏览器插件和网络爬虫等实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体平台上博彩应用广告数量显著增长，这些广告常使用可能误导用户、鼓励风险行为并影响心理健康的说服技巧。然而，由于缺乏公开标注的数据集，自动检测操纵性和欺骗性博彩广告的研究仍十分有限。

Method: 从Instagram和Reddit两个社交媒体平台收集博彩广告数据，进行人工标注，识别操纵性和欺骗性广告实践，并提供标注推理的解释。此外，分析博彩广告中常用的策略及其对心理健康的潜在影响。

Result: 构建了一个包含人工标注和解释的博彩广告数据集，揭示了博彩广告中常见的说服策略及其对心理健康的潜在影响。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于开发浏览器插件以警告用户操纵性博彩广告，以及自动化网络爬虫帮助监管机构监控和检测此类在线推广活动。

Abstract: The promotion of betting applications on social media platforms has increased significantly in recent years. Many of these advertisements use persuasive techniques that may mislead users, encourage risky behavior, and potentially influence users' mental well-being. However, research on the automated detection of manipulative and deceptive betting advertisements remains limited due to the lack of publicly available annotated datasets. In this work, we introduce a new dataset of betting-related advertisements collected from two widely used social media platforms, Instagram and Reddit. The advertisements were manually annotated for manipulative and deceptive advertising practices. In addition to classification labels, the dataset includes human-provided explanations that describe the reasoning behind each annotation, enabling research into explainable approaches to detecting manipulative advertising. Furthermore, we analyze the strategies commonly used in betting advertisements and examine how these persuasive tactics may impact users' mental health. The proposed framework can also enable practical applications such as browser plugins that warn users about manipulative betting advertisements and automated web crawlers that help regulatory authorities monitor and detect such promotions online.

</details>


### [86] [How Good Can Linear Models Be for Time-Series Forecasting?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27282)
*Lang Huang,Jinglue Xu,Luke Darlow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战时间序列预测中“更大模型带来更高精度”的主流观点，通过调优预处理（上下文长度、归一化、正则化、数据增强）而非扩大模型规模，使用Ridge回归作为测试平台，在8个基准上超越Transformer、MLP、CNN等大型模型，并发现三个关键模式：最优回溯长度与预测步长呈非单调关系、局部归一化优于全局归一化、跨序列超参数共享程度因数据集而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战时间序列预测研究中“更大架构（从专用Transformer到通用基础模型）才能提升精度”的主流假设，探究能否通过更经济的预处理调优来缩小性能差距。

Method: 使用Ridge回归（因其闭式解和可解释权重）作为测试平台，对上下文长度、局部归一化、正则化和数据增强进行超参数搜索，在8个标准基准上评估。

Result: (1) 最优回溯长度高度序列特异且与预测步长呈非单调关系，幂律指数从+0.46到-0.19不等；(2) 基于学习到的上下文末尾比例的局部归一化几乎普遍更优；(3) 同一数据集内不同序列的最优超参数不同，跨序列共享程度从完全共享到完全独立。优化后的模型在大多数数据集-预测步长组合上超越线性模型，并在6/8个基准上超过Transformer/MLP/CNN基线。

Conclusion: 预处理调优能以更低成本达到接近甚至超越大型模型的效果；优化后的超参数可作为数据诊断工具，揭示大型模型隐式吸收的数据结构。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting research has been moving steadily toward larger architectures, from specialized transformers to general-purpose foundation models, on the assumption that capacity is what unlocks accuracy. We take the opposite position: most of the gap can be closed at far lower cost by tuning preprocessing rather than scaling models. We use Ridge regression as the testbed, since it has a closed-form solution and interpretable weights, which let the optimal hyperparameters be read off the search directly. We search over context length, local normalization, regularization, and augmentation on eight standard benchmarks and find three patterns. (1) Optimal lookback is strongly series-specific and often non-monotonic in forecast horizon, with fitted power-law exponents ranging from $+0.46$ on ETTm2 to $-0.19$ on Exchange and Traffic, challenging the convention that longer horizons need longer history. (2) Normalizing over a learned trailing fraction of the context, rather than its entirety, is almost universally preferred. (3) Series within the same dataset often disagree on hyperparameters; the optimal degree of cross-series sharing varies from fully shared to fully per-series. The resulting models beat prior linear forecasters on most dataset-horizon entries and exceed Transformer, MLP, and CNN baselines on six of eight benchmarks. The optimized hyperparameters also serve as a diagnostic on the data itself, revealing structures that larger models absorb silently into their learned parameters.

</details>


### [87] [Recovering Governing Equations from Solution Data: Identifiability Bounds for Linear and Nonlinear ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27285)
*Yang Pan,Helmut Bölcskei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对从解观测数据中学习控制方程的理论挑战，引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的自然度量，建立了线性到非线性ODE类的可辨识性界，并推导了样本复杂度下界，首次量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需的解观测数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学机器学习方法在从观测数据中发现控制方程方面取得了进展，但关于真实ODE能否从多个解观测中唯一且稳定地辨识的理论条件仍不成熟，且缺乏样本复杂度的定量分析。

Method: 引入解集上的Hausdorff距离作为比较微分方程的度量，该距离通过所有容许初始条件上的最坏情况分离来编码辨识问题的极小极大结构；在此基础上推导熵估计并分析样本复杂度界。

Result: 建立了从线性ODE到具有Lipschitz/ Hölder连续向量场的非线性类等广泛结构方程的可辨识性界，并量化了可靠恢复控制方程所需解观测数量的样本复杂度上界。

Conclusion: Hausdorff距离为ODE辨识问题提供了合适的极小极大框架，所建立的熵估计和样本复杂度结果为科学机器学习中控制方程发现任务提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Learning governing equations from observed solution data is a fundamental challenge in scientific machine learning \cite{bruntonDiscoveringGoverningEquations2016,kovachkiNeuralOperatorLearning2023,longPDENetLearningPDEs2018,rudyDatadrivenDiscoveryPartial2017,raonicConvolutionalNeuralOperators2023}, yet the theoretical conditions under which a ground-truth ODE can be uniquely and stably identified from multiple solution observations remain largely undeveloped, and no quantitative analysis of the sample complexity of such learning tasks exists in the literature. To address this gap, we introduce the Hausdorff distance on solution sets as the natural metric for comparing differential equations, since it captures the worst-case separation between two equations over all admissible initial conditions and thus encodes the minimax structure of the identification problem. We establish identifiability bounds for governing ODEs across a wide class of structure equations--ranging from linear ODEs to nonlinear classes with Lipschitz (Hölder)-continuous vector fields--characterizing precisely when two distinct equations can be distinguished from solution data. Using this metric, we derive metric entropy estimates for the relevant ODE classes and analyze sample complexity bounds, quantifying how many solution observations are needed to reliably recover the governing equation.

</details>


### [88] [Designing Reward Signals for Portable Query Generation: A Case Study in Industrial Semantic Job Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27291)
*Ping Liu,Qianqi Shen,Jianqiang Shen,Wenqiong Liu,Rajat Arora,Yunxiang Ren,Chunnan Yao,Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Wanjun Jiang,Andrii Soviak,Kevin Kao,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端RLAIF框架来生成可移植的求职搜索查询，并通过实验证明：训练成功根本上取决于奖励塑形（reward shaping）而非优化器选择，其中GRPO因组相对优势归一化而对奖励信号异常敏感，易被利用导致退化行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求职搜索平台依赖低带宽查询接口，难以捕捉候选简历的高维复杂性。现有方法无法生成既能抽象掉求职者个人标识、又能保留通用资质特征的“可移植”搜索查询词。

Method: 提出端到端RLAIF框架生成可移植查询词；引入确定性、基于规则的奖励下限来纠正逐字复制行为的奖励；系统比较了RLOO、REINFORCE++和GRPO等优化器在不同奖励塑形下的表现。

Result: 无评论家的优化器其性能完全由奖励塑形决定，优化器选择本身不重要；GRPO因组相对优势归一化对虚假奖励信号异常敏感；引入规则奖励下限后质量提升+0.147；训练时奖励模型将性能增益膨胀了2.4倍。

Conclusion: 训练成功从根本上取决于奖励塑形纪律的强制执行，而非优化器的选择；GRPO在对抗性奖励表面下存在固有的脆弱性。

Abstract: Job-search platforms rely on low-bandwidth query interfaces that often fail to capture the high-dimensional complexity of candidate profiles. We present an end-to-end RLAIF (Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback) framework to generate \emph{portable} job search queries, terms that abstract away seeker-specific identifiers while preserving generalizable qualifications. This task introduces a highly adversarial reward surface where policy optimization frequently exploits flaws in LLM-as-judge rubrics, resulting in degenerate verbatim-copying behaviors.
  We conducted comprehensive empirical experiments to isolate the impact of optimization mechanics against structured reward engineering. Our results demonstrate that for critic-free optimizers, performance is overwhelmingly dictated by robust reward shaping, rendering the specific choice of algorithm largely immaterial. While critic-free per-rollout baseline methods (RLOO and REINFORCE++) natively resist reward-hacking, the group-relative advantage normalization in GRPO appears uniquely sensitive to spurious reward signals, making it disproportionately susceptible to exploitation. We show that introducing a deterministic, rule-based reward floor to correct for rewards assigned to verbatim copying mitigates this failure mode, resulting in a substantial $+0.147$ quality improvement on a cross-family evaluation judge. Ultimately, we show that the training-time reward model inflates performance gains by $2.4\times$, confirming that the training success is fundamentally dependent on enforcing reward-shaping disciplines rather than selecting alternative optimizers.

</details>


### [89] [A Multi-Fidelity Convolutional Autoencoder-Transfer Learning Framework for Guided-Wave-Based Damage Diagnosis Using Large Simulated and Limited Experimental Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27304)
*Santosh Kapuria,Abhishek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多保真迁移学习框架，结合轻量物理仿真、卷积自编码器深度特征学习、前馈神经网络和少量实验数据，实现板状结构中损伤的精确定位与定量，在计算效率和预测精度上优于传统CNN方法，R²分别达到0.93（定位）和0.99（定量）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决导波结构健康监测中深度学习模型因标记实验数据稀缺和大规模高保真仿真计算成本高昂而难以实际部署的问题。

Method: 采用一维时域谱元模型生成大规模合成数据集进行预训练，利用卷积自编码器进行深度特征学习，结合前馈神经网络，并通过迁移学习仅用少量标记实验数据将模型适配到实验域。

Result: CAE迁移学习框架在损伤定位精度上显著优于CNN对应方法，R²评分超过0.93（定位）和0.99（定量），并在未见过的损伤场景上展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 提出的框架是一种准确、计算高效且实际可行的解决方案，适用于真实世界的导波结构健康监测应用。

Abstract: Guided wave-based structural health monitoring (GWSHM) with onboard transducers offers significant potential for the early diagnosis of damage in engineering structures. However, the practical deployment of deep learning models is often hindered by the limited availability of labelled experimental data and the high computational cost of generating large-scale high-fidelity simulation datasets. This study presents a multifidelity transfer learning framework that integrates lightweight physics-based simulations, convolutional autoencoder (CAE)-based deep feature learning, a feed-forward neural network, and limited experimental measurements for accurate damage localisation and sizing in plate-like structures instrumented with piezoelectric transducers. A computationally efficient one-dimensional time-domain spectral element model is employed to generate a large synthetic dataset for pretraining, while transfer learning adapts the model to experimental domains using only a small amount of labelled data. The CAE-based transfer learning framework significantly outperforms its CNN-based counterpart in damage localisation accuracy. The model achieves excellent predictive performance with $R^2$ scores exceeding 0.93 for damage localisation and 0.99 for damage sizing. Its generalisation capability is demonstrated on previously unseen data, showing high prediction accuracy for damage scenarios not represented during pretraining or fine-tuning. The results establish the proposed framework as an accurate, computationally efficient, and practically viable solution for real-world GWSHM applications.

</details>


### [90] [Blackwell Approachability and Gradient Equilibrium are Equivalent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27315)
*Brian W. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Michael I. Jordan,Ryan J. Tibshirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明梯度均衡（GEQ）与Blackwell可逼近性在算法上等价，进而通过已知等价关系表明GEQ与遗憾最小化、校准等框架等价，并建立了高效的归约与转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作表明GEQ误差与遗憾目标不可比较，缺乏对GEQ在在线学习框架中定位的精确理解，因此需要阐明GEQ与已有框架（如遗憾最小化）的关系。

Method: 通过构建GEQ oracle与Blackwell可逼近性之间的双向归约（无渐进误差损失），利用已知的可逼近性与遗憾最小化、校准之间的等价关系，推导GEQ与这些框架的等价性；同时识别GEQ的充要条件，建立约束与无约束决策集下不同GEQ概念间的归约。

Result: GEQ与Blackwell可逼近性在算法上等价，进而与遗憾最小化、校准等价；能够将乐观性、强自适应性等精细保证从遗憾最小化迁移至GEQ。

Conclusion: GEQ并非独立于已有在线学习框架，而是与Blackwell可逼近性、遗憾最小化、校准等核心框架在算法上等价，统一了在线学习的理论图景。

Abstract: Gradient equilibrium (GEQ) is a recently introduced online optimization framework that generalizes first-order stationarity from offline optimization and abstracts problems like online conformal prediction. While GEQ has curious similarities with known online learning frameworks, namely regret minimization, prior work has shown that GEQ error and regret are incomparable objectives, leaving open a precise understanding of how GEQ fits into the broader online learning landscape. In this work, we show that GEQ is equivalent to Blackwell approachability in the algorithmic sense. That is, a Blackwell approachability problem can always be solved using queries to a black-box GEQ oracle, with no asymptotic loss in the oracle's error rate, and vice versa. Taken together with known equivalences between approachability, regret minimization, and calibration, these results imply that GEQ is equivalent to these frameworks, as well. Our reductions are efficient and can be used to transfer refined guarantees, such as optimism and strong adaptivity, from regret minimization to GEQ. Along the way, we also identify necessary and sufficient conditions for GEQ, and establish reductions between different notions of GEQ with unconstrained and constrained decision sets.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond the Hard Budget: Sparsity Regularizers for More Interpretable Top-k Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27321)
*Nathanaël Jacquier,Maria Vakalopoulou,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种与Top-k稀疏自编码器兼容的稀疏正则化方法（ℓ1惩罚和ℓ1/ℓ2比例惩罚），通过作用于Top-k选择前的激活值，在不损失重构质量的前提下提升视觉基础模型特征的单义性，证明了硬架构稀疏性与软稀疏正则化的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Top-k SAE虽避免使用ℓ1惩罚，但存在固定预算k无法适应输入复杂度、易过拟合到训练k值等局限，作者旨在引入软稀疏正则化来弥补这些缺陷。

Method: 在Top-k选择前的激活上施加两种稀疏正则化：(1) 对未选中单元的ℓ1惩罚；(2) 尺度不变的ℓ1/ℓ2比率惩罚。两种惩罚仅作用于批次活跃单元（批次内至少被Top-k选中一次的单元）。

Result: 在2个数据集、3个视觉基础模型和多种k值下，两种正则化均一致提升单义性而不损失重构质量；ℓ1/ℓ2惩罚进一步将信息集中在更少的隐变量上，增强了对推理时k选择的鲁棒性，并提升了小预算线性探针性能。

Conclusion: 硬架构稀疏性（Top-k）与软稀疏正则化是互补而非互斥的，二者结合可兼得二者的优势。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a leading tool for interpreting the representations of vision foundation models, decomposing their polysemantic activations into a larger set of sparse, more monosemantic features. The Top-$k$ SAE, a now-standard variant, enforces sparsity architecturally through its activation function, retaining only the $k$ most active latents per input. Because it was designed precisely to avoid the $\ell_1$ penalty used by earlier SAEs and its known drawbacks, it has not been combined with an explicit sparsity regularizer, despite retaining limitations of its own, such as a budget $k$ that is fixed regardless of input complexity and a tendency to overfit to the training value of $k$. We introduce two sparsity regularizers compatible with the Top-$k$ architecture, both acting on the activations before the Top-$k$ selection: an $\ell_1$ penalty on the unselected (off-support) units, and a scale-invariant $\ell_1/\ell_2$-ratio penalty that concentrates the code onto fewer effective units. Both penalties are applied only to the batch-active units, those selected by the Top-$k$ operator at least once within the batch. Across two datasets, three vision foundation models, and a range of $k$, both regularizers consistently improve monosemanticity at no cost to reconstruction quality. The $\ell_1/\ell_2$ penalty further concentrates information into fewer latents, making reconstruction more robust to the inference-time choice of $k$ and improving small-budget linear probing. Our central finding is that hard architectural sparsity and soft sparsity regularization are complementary rather than mutually exclusive.

</details>


### [92] [Hallucination in World Models is Predictable and Preventable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27326)
*Nicklas Hansen,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出幻觉本质是数据覆盖问题，通过MMBench2数据集和覆盖感知采样技术，实现了对生成世界模型中幻觉的检测与缓解，仅需50条真实轨迹即可微调模型适应全新环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式世界模型虽能生成逼真的可控未来，但常出现幻觉（视觉流畅但动态偏离真实），作者假设幻觉集中在状态-动作空间的低覆盖区域，并希望用轻量级数据驱动信号检测并引导缓解。

Method: 构建427小时、210任务的MMBench2数据集，训练350M参数世界模型；识别三种幻觉模式（感知、动作边缘化、场景发散）；开发三种预测信号；提出覆盖感知采样技术用于训练时，并利用幻觉预测器作为好奇心奖励指导在线数据收集。

Result: 所提信号能准确预测模型失败位置；覆盖感知采样和基于好奇心奖励的数据收集策略，使得预训练世界模型仅需50条真实轨迹即可适应全新环境。

Conclusion: 世界模型中的幻觉本质上是数据覆盖问题，用于检测幻觉的信号同样可以用于缓解幻觉。

Abstract: Modern generative world models render increasingly realistic action-controllable futures, yet they frequently hallucinate: rollouts remain visually fluent while drifting from the ground-truth dynamics. We hypothesize that hallucination concentrates in low-coverage regions of the state-action space, where lightweight data-centric signals can both detect it and guide mitigation. To test this, we introduce MMBench2, a 427-hour, 210-task dataset for visual world modeling with ground-truth actions, rewards, and live simulators, and train a 350M-parameter world model on it. We identify three distinct hallucination modes: perceptual, action-marginalized, and scene-diverging -- each anchored to a different stage of the pipeline, and develop three signals that accurately predict where the model will fail. To close coverage gaps at training time, we develop a coverage-aware sampling technique; to close them online, our hallucination predictors serve as curiosity rewards for targeted data collection, yielding a data-efficient finetuning recipe that adapts the pretrained world model to entirely unseen environments with as few as 50 real environment trajectories. Overall, our findings reveal that hallucination in world models is inherently a data coverage issue, and that the same signals used to detect it can also be used for mitigation.
  An interactive web version of our paper is available at https://www.nicklashansen.com/mmbench2

</details>


### [93] [Error-Conditioned Neural Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27354)
*Haina Jiang,Liam Wang,Peng-Chen Chen,Min Seop Kwak,Seungryong Kim,Brian Bell,Jeong Joon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出误差条件神经求解器（ENS），通过将PDE残差场作为网络输入而非优化目标，使模型能读取自身错误结构并学习迭代修正策略，在四个PDE系列中实现更高预测精度（湍流Kolmogorov流提升10倍），且在分布偏移下具有强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经代理模型无法修正自身约束违反和外推能力差；混合方法虽通过残差优化提升物理正确性，但在病态系统中残差最小化与重建精度不一致且计算成本高。

Method: 将PDE残差场作为网络直接输入，使模型在每个迭代步读取自身误差的空间结构，学习一个更新策略来迭代修正预测结果，而非将残差作为优化目标。

Result: 在四个PDE系列中，ENS在大多数设置下达到最高预测精度，湍流Kolmogorov流提升达10倍；在分布偏移（零样本参数变化、跨方程迁移）下优势更大，尤其在病态系统中残差最小化最不可靠的区域。

Conclusion: 数值最小化PDE残差在病态系统中可能不可靠；ENS通过将残差作为输入特征而非优化目标，实现了更准确、更高效的预测，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural surrogate models offer fast approximate mappings from PDE parameters to solutions, but they typically treat solving as a purely statistical task: once trained, they struggle to correct their own constraint violations and extrapolate beyond the training distribution. Recent hybrid methods promote physical correctness by targeting the PDE residual via gradient descent or Gauss--Newton steps, but inherit the compute cost and instability of the underlying classical optimizers. We show, theoretically and empirically, that numerically minimizing the PDE residual can be an unreliable proxy for reconstruction accuracy in ill-conditioned systems, explaining why these methods often do not make accurate predictions despite achieving low residuals. We propose error-conditioned Neural Solvers (ENS), built on a different principle: rather than an optimization target, the PDE residual field is passed as a direct input to the network at each iteration, enabling it to read the spatial structure of its own errors and learn an update policy to iteratively correct its predictions. Across four PDE families, ENS attains the highest prediction accuracy in the large majority of settings, with gains reaching $10\times$ on turbulent Kolmogorov flow, while avoiding the expensive compute cost of hybrid methods. ENS's learned correction policy generalizes under distribution shift, including zero-shot parameter changes and cross-equation transfer, where its relative advantage is largest in the ill-conditioned regimes where residual minimization is least reliable. Project website: https://neuralsolver.github.io/.

</details>


### [94] [Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27361)
*Danyal Rehman,Charlie B. Tan,Yoshua Bengio,Avishek Joey Bose,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归玻尔兹曼生成器（ArBG），通过自回归建模框架替代基于流的范式，克服了归一化流在表达性和计算成本上的局限，在分子平衡态采样任务上显著优于现有方法，并训练了1.32亿参数的可迁移模型Robin，在零样本能量误差上降低超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有玻尔兹曼生成器主要依赖归一化流，但离散时间流受严格可逆约束导致表达性受限，连续时间流则面临计算昂贵的似然估计问题，亟需一种能突破这些拓扑限制的新框架。

Method: 采用自回归建模框架替代基于流的范式，结合大语言模型中有效的架构提升可扩展性，支持推理时的顺序干预，同时规避流的拓扑约束。

Result: 在所有基准测试上显著优于基于流的模型，特别是在大型肽系统（如10残基的Chignolin）上表现突出；Robin模型在8残基系统的零样本能量误差（E-W₂）上降低超过60%。

Conclusion: 自回归框架为分子平衡态采样提供了一条超越归一化流的新路径，在大规模可迁移模型上取得了实质性的性能突破。

Abstract: Efficient sampling of molecular systems at thermodynamic equilibrium is a hallmark challenge in statistical physics. This challenge has driven the development of Boltzmann Generators (BGs), which allow rapid generation of uncorrelated equilibrium samples by combining a generative model with exact likelihoods and an importance sampling correction. However, modern BGs predominantly rely on normalizing flows (NFs), which either suffer from limited expressivity due to strict invertibility constraints (discrete time) or computationally expensive likelihoods (continuous time). In this paper, we propose Autoregressive Boltzmann Generators (ArBG) -- a novel autoregressive modelling framework -- that overcomes these limitations by departing from the flow-based BG paradigm. ArBG circumvents the topological constraints of flows and enables sequential inference-time interventions, while offering enhanced scalability by leveraging architectures effective in Large Language Models. We empirically demonstrate that ArBG leads to significant improvements over flow-based models across all benchmarks, but particularly in larger peptide systems such as the 10-residue Chignolin. Furthermore, we introduce Robin, a 132 million parameter transferable model trained with the ArBG framework which improves over the previous state-of-the-art, reducing the zero-shot energy error, E-W$_2$, on 8-residue systems by over 60$\%$. The code can be found at the following link: https://github.com/danyalrehman/autobg.

</details>


### [95] [Reinforcement Learning without Ground-Truth Solutions can Improve LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27369)
*Yingyu Lin,Qiyue Gao,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Xunpeng Huang,Kun Zhou,Tongtong Liang,Zhewei Yao,Yi-An Ma,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RiVER框架，通过标量执行反馈对LLM进行基于分数的优化训练，无需真实答案。针对连续奖励下的"尺度主导"和"频率主导"问题，RiVER采用实例级比较校准奖励并强调最优解。在12个AtCoder任务上训练后，RiVER在算法工程基准上提升约9%，并意外地在精确解基准（LiveCodeBench、USACO）上也有2.4%和3.5%的提升，而基线方法无法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖真实答案来分配奖励，限制了其在真实解未知的任务（如基于分数的优化任务）中的应用。因此需要一种无需真实解即可训练LLM进行分数优化的方法。

Method: 提出RiVER框架，核心包括：(1) 使用确定性执行反馈作为连续值监督；(2) 识别组相对RL应用于连续奖励时的两个问题——尺度主导（未校准分数跨度扭曲策略更新）和频率主导（重复采样次优解压倒罕见但更强的解）；(3) 通过实例级比较进行校准奖励塑造，并强调排名靠前的求解器，同时为其他有效解保留有界反馈。

Result: 在12个AtCoder竞赛任务上训练，评估于ALE-Bench、LiveCodeBench和USACO。RiVER使Qwen3-8B和GLM-Z1-9B-0414在ALE评分排名上分别提升8.9%和9.4%。更重要的是，在精确解基准上分别提升2.4%（LiveCodeBench）和3.5%（USACO），而基线方法仅提升ALE评分但无法迁移。

Conclusion: 基于分数的优化任务结合适当的奖励校准，可以在没有真实解的情况下作为通用编码能力的有效训练环境。RiVER表明连续奖励校准能够实现从分数任务到精确解任务的迁移学习。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for training LLMs typically rely on ground-truth answers to assign rewards, limiting their applicability to tasks where the ground-truth solution is unknown. We introduce a \textbf{R}anking-\textbf{i}nduced \textbf{VER}ifiable framework (RiVER) that trains LLMs on score-based optimization tasks without ground-truth solutions, using deterministic execution feedback as continuous-valued supervision. When applying group-relative RL to such continuous rewards, we identify two key challenges: \emph{scale dominance}, where uncalibrated score magnitudes across test instances distort policy updates, and \emph{frequency dominance}, where repeatedly sampled suboptimal solutions can outweigh rare but stronger candidates. RiVER addresses these challenges with calibrated reward shaping that uses instance-wise comparisons and emphasizes top-ranked solvers while retaining bounded feedback for other valid solutions. We train on 12 AtCoder Heuristic Contest tasks and evaluate on Algorithm Engineering Benchmark (ALE-Bench), LiveCodeBench, and USACO. RiVER advances Qwen3-8B and GLM-Z1-9B-0414 by 8.9\% and 9.4\% in ALE rating rank. More importantly, despite training exclusively on score-based tasks without any ground-truth solutions, RiVER also improves the backbones across exact-solution benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench and USACO by an absolute average improvement of 2.4\% and 3.5\%. By contrast, baselines trained with raw execution scores improve ALE rating but fail to transfer to exact-solution benchmarks. These results suggest that score-based optimization tasks, combined with proper reward calibration, can serve as effective training environments for general coding ability without ground-truth solutions.

</details>

### [96] [Dissipative Quantum Multiplicative Weights with Sampling Feedback: A Classically Hard Primitive Realized via Engineered Open-System Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26162)
*Agung Trisetyarso,Lenny Putri Yulianti,Kridanto Surendro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出DQMW-Sample，一种利用开放量子系统动力学实现在线学习的原始方法，通过工程化Davies型耗散器制备吉布斯态，在计算复杂性上证明量子优势：该算法实现渐近次线性遗憾，而所有高效经典学习器在特定实例上遭受恒定平均遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将恒定温度吉布斯采样的计算困难性转化为物理可实现的在线学习原语，寻求在线学习中基于复杂性理论且与近超导硬件兼容的计算优势路径。

Method: 工程化Davies型耗散器，其每轮反馈在经典上不能被高效模拟；通过耗散量子乘法权重算法（DQMW-Sample），在每一轮通过计算基测量提供损失反馈；采用平衡耗散调度以控制硬件噪声。

Result: DQMW-Sample实现渐近次线性遗憾，而经典学习器遭遇常数平均遗憾；证明了工程化耗散器的谱隙能够抑制硬件噪声，产生次线性噪声诱导遗憾；证明整个T轮反馈过程的高效经典模拟将导致多项式层级坍缩；在IBM Heron r2处理器上进行了初步硬件表征。

Conclusion: DQMW-Sample为在线学习中实现计算优势提供了具体路径，该优势基于复杂性理论且与近超导量子硬件兼容。

Abstract: We introduce \emph{Dissipative Quantum Multiplicative Weights with Sampling Feedback} (DQMW-Sample), an online-learning primitive in which engineered open quantum-system dynamics prepare a Gibbs state whose computational-basis measurement supplies the loss feedback. The central conceptual contribution is to lift the computational hardness of constant-temperature Gibbs sampling into a physically realizable online-learning primitive. By engineering a Davies-type dissipator whose per-round feedback cannot be efficiently simulated classically, we obtain a learning-theoretic separation in which DQMW-Sample achieves asymptotically sublinear regret while every efficient classical learner suffers constant average regret on a suitably constructed instance. We further prove that the spectral gap of the engineered dissipator contracts hardware noise, yielding sublinear noise-induced regret under a balanced dissipation schedule, and we strengthen the single-round hardness to the full adaptive interaction: an efficient classical simulator of the entire $T$-round feedback process would collapse the polynomial hierarchy. We state the required realizability assumption in explicit form and report an initial hardware characterization on the IBM Heron~r2 processor. These results position DQMW-Sample as a concrete route toward computational advantage in online learning that is grounded in complexity theory and compatible with near-term superconducting hardware.

</details>


### [97] [Criterion for qubit-assisted quantum metrology approaching Heisenberg scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26167)
*Peng Chen,Jun Jing*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了与辅助量子比特耦合的探测系统的计量精度，发现仅沿一维或二维方向限制探测-量子比特耦合是实现海森堡极限精度的充分条件。在此条件下，量子Fisher信息变成复合系统初态下有效发生器均方值的期望。分别在玻色子探针和自旋系综探针两个系统中验证了该准则，前者发现位移估计的QFI与探针平均激发数成正比，得出提高探针温度可增强量子计量灵敏度的反直觉结论；后者发现旋转相位和磁场估计的QFI均与探针自旋数呈二次依赖关系，即使自旋系综处于有限温度态而非压缩态或GHZ态等资源态，QFI仍可表现出海森堡标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究与辅助量子比特耦合的探针系统在非理想条件下（如有限温度）能否突破标准量子极限、达到海森堡极限精度，并建立可实现该极限的充分判据。

Method: 采用量子Fisher信息（QFI）作为度量精度的核心工具，通过将探针-量子比特耦合限制在特定方向上（一维或二维）来构造有效动力学发生器，推导出QFI等于有效发生器均方值的期望。在玻色子探针（位移估计）和自旋系综探针（旋转相位和磁场估计）两个系统中验证该准则。

Result: 1) 玻色子探针：QFI与探针平均激发数成正比，提高温度反而增强灵敏度（反直觉）。2) 自旋系综探针：QFI与探针自旋数呈二次关系（海森堡标度），且有限温度态下仍保持该标度行为。

Conclusion: 通过限制探针-辅助量子比特的耦合方向，可以在非玻色子/非纯态条件下实现海森堡极限精度，且无需依赖压缩态或GHZ态等传统资源态，为量子计量在实际系统中的实现提供了更宽松的条件。

Abstract: We study in this work the metrology precision of a probe system coupled to an ancillary qubit. Restricting the probe-qubit coupling along only one or two directions is found to be a sufficient criterion for the effective dynamical generator to achieve the Heisenberg limit in precision. Under the criterion, the quantum Fisher information (QFI) about the to-be-estimated parameter becomes the expectation value of mean square of the effective generator with respect to the initial state of the composite system. Our criterion is justified in two distinct systems. For a bosonic probe, QFI about the displacement estimation is found to be proportional to the mean excitation number of probe. It renders a counterintuitive result that quantum metrology sensitivity can be enhanced by increasing the temperature of the probe system. For a spin-ensemble probe, QFI about both rotation-phase and magnetic-field estimation exhibit a quadratic dependence on the probe-spin number. It is found that even when the spin-ensemble is prepared as a finite-temperature state, faraway from the so-called resource states, e.g., the squeezed state or the Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state, QFI can still manifest a Heisenberg scaling behavior.

</details>


### [98] [Interpretable rule-based learning in an autonomous thermodynamic network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26220)
*Sparrow Suderman,Giulia Rubino*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于热力学神经元（自主量子热机）构建的随机Tsetlin机分类器，利用噪声、耗散和不可逆性作为计算资源，在无需外部时间控制的情况下实现与标准Tsetlin机统计相当的分类精度，展示了自主随机动力学如何产生可解释的学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习基于确定性逻辑运算，而物理系统天然存在噪声、耗散和不可逆性。作者旨在将这些物理效应转化为计算资源，构建自主、可解释的学习架构。

Method: 使用热力学神经元（自主量子热机）构建逻辑门（AND、NOT、OR），并结合自主耦合机制，构建随机版本的Tsetlin机分类器。整个计算过程无需外部时间相关控制，依靠热流驱动逻辑操作。

Result: 尽管组件存在噪声，该分类器在分类精度上与标准Tsetlin机统计可比。可靠性来源于阈值处理和冗余等架构机制，而非精确的逻辑运算。

Conclusion: 准确且可解释的学习可以从自主随机动力学中涌现，热力学计算可作为物理机器学习的一个可行框架。

Abstract: Machine learning is typically described in terms of deterministic logical operations, whereas physical systems generally operate in the presence of noise, dissipation and irreversibility. Here, we turn these physical effects into computational resources for an autonomous, interpretable learning architecture. We develop a classifier based on thermodynamic neurons, which are autonomous quantum thermal machines that implement logical operations through heat flow, and use these to construct a stochastic version of the Tsetlin machine, an interpretable rule-based learning architecture. By combining thermodynamic AND, NOT and OR gates with an autonomous coupling mechanism, we realise a learning system whose computation unfolds without the need for external time-dependent control. Despite its noisy components, the resulting classifier achieves classification accuracy that is statistically comparable to that of the standard Tsetlin machine. Reliability arises from architectural mechanisms such as thresholding and redundancy, rather than exact logical operations. Our results highlight that accurate and interpretable learning can emerge from autonomous stochastic dynamics, and establish thermodynamic computation as a viable framework for physical machine learning.

</details>


### [99] [A fidelity metric for quantum annealing benchmarked by extreme scaling quantum Monte-Carlo simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26233)
*Gabriel Gouraud,Miha Srdinsek,Xavier Waintal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种衡量量子退火过程质量的新指标——退火精度ε（类似于门量子计算机的每门保真度），并通过两种模拟量子退火方法对里德伯原子系统进行计算。变分量子蒙特卡洛方法在单CPU上可处理多达1亿个原子，精度达10⁻²-10⁻³；格林函数量子蒙特卡洛方法可处理10万个原子，精度达10⁻⁴。这些结果在精度和规模上远超当前里德伯原子量子退火实验平台，对未来硬件提出了严峻约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子退火器性能评估过度依赖特定优化问题的求解效果和经典基准算法，这种评估方法本质上是脆弱的。作者希望提出一个衡量量子退火过程本身质量的通用指标，而非仅关注最终结果。

Method: 提出量子退火精度ε作为退火器状态方程的精度度量（类比门量子计算机的每门保真度）。采用两种模拟量子退火方法：(1) 变分量子蒙特卡洛（VQMC），使用热退火启发的试探波函数；(2) 格林函数量子蒙特卡洛（GFQMC）。

Result: VQMC方法在单CPU上可处理高达1亿个原子，精度ε在10⁻²-10⁻³量级；GFQMC方法可处理10万个原子，精度ε约10⁻⁴。两种方法在精度和系统规模上均显著超越当前里德伯原子量子退火实验平台。

Conclusion: 所提出的精度指标能有效评估量子退火过程质量，经典模拟方法在精度和规模上远超现有实验硬件，为未来量子退火器的性能提升设立了高标准。

Abstract: Quantum annealers are supposed to follow adiabatically the ground state of a system as its Hamiltonian slowly interpolates between a trivial phase and a non-trivial one; the non-trivial ground state being the solution to an optimization problem. Overwhelmingly, their performances are measured in terms of how well or fast the optimization problem is solved. While pragmatic, this approach is inherently brittle as it strongly depends on the problem considered and the classical algorithm used as the reference benchmark. Here, we propose a quantity that not only measures the end result but also the quality of the actual quantum annealing process itself. Our metric is the quantum annealing counterpart of the fidelity-per gate of gate-based quantum computers. It takes the form of an accuracy $ε$ for the equation of state of the annealer. We calculate benchmark values of $ε$ using two variants of the simulated quantum annealing technique for Rydberg atoms systems. Our first approach uses variational quantum Monte-Carlo with an ansatz inspired by thermal annealing. It suggests that within $ε\sim 10^{-2}-10^{-3}$, a quantum annealer is indistinguishable from its thermal classical counterpart. Critically, we could reach this precision up to $100,000,000$ atoms on a single CPU. Our second approach (based on Green function quantum Monte-Carlo) reaches accuracies around $ε\sim 10^{-4}$ and we have run it up to $100,000$ atoms. These results outperform current Rydberg atom quantum annealing experimental platforms in both precision and size by orders of magnitude and put severe constraints for future hardware.

</details>


### [100] [Measures of Chirality in Mixed-State Topological Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26235)
*Shijun Sun,Bader Aldossari,Rasmit Devkota,Zhu-Xi Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究混合态拓扑相的手征性诊断问题，指出传统纯态诊断方法（体-边界对应、纠缠谱、模交换子、热霍尔效应）在混合态中均不可靠，并提出基于相对熵的新诊断方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态拓扑相的手征性在数学上可通过对称代数精确刻画，但物理上缺乏可靠的诊断工具。纯态中一系列成熟的手征性诊断方法在混合态中是否仍然有效？这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 理论分析纯态手征性诊断在混合态中的失效机制，提出两种基于相对熵的诊断度量，其中一种可提取手征中心荷。

Result: 发现体-边界对应、纠缠谱、模交换子、热霍尔效应等纯态诊断在混合态中均不再可靠；提出基于相对熵的新度量可有效诊断混合态手征性。

Conclusion: 混合态拓扑相需要全新的、超越纯态探针直接类比的内在诊断方案。

Abstract: What does it mean for a mixed-state topological phase to be chiral? Mathematically, chirality can be sharply characterized through the symmetry algebra of the mixed state. Physically, however, the question is far more subtle. In pure states, chirality in topological phase is tied to a web of familiar diagnostics, involving bulk-boundary correspondence, a gapless entanglement spectrum, a nontrivial modular commutator, and a quantized thermal Hall response. We show that none of these diagnostics remain reliable in mixed states. Instead, for decohered topological phases with a known error-free parent state, we propose two relative-entropy-based measures that can diagnose chirality, with one of them further extracting the chiral central charge. Our results emphasize how mixed-state topology demands intrinsically new diagnostics beyond direct analogues of pure-state probes.

</details>


### [101] [Permutation asymmetry unlocks emergent advantage in randomized Bell tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26242)
*Wieslaw Laskowski,Tamas Vertesi,Jan Wojcik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在随机化贝尔实验中，最大纠缠两量子比特态的非局域性表现取决于其置换对称性：置换不变态在两个实验中产生相同的非局域性结果，而非对称态可能在一个实验中违反局域实在论而在另一个中不违反，这可用于检测测量选择的相关性，并在有限测量池中显著提高观测到非局域性的概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管所有最大纠缠两量子比特态在均匀随机投影测量下违反局域实在论的概率相同，但它们在随机化贝尔测试中的行为未必一致。本文旨在探究当测量设置在顺序贝尔实验中交换时，共享态的置换对称性如何决定两种实现之间的统计关系，并揭示置换不对称性在随机化贝尔实验中的有用性。

Method: 通过理论分析，研究在顺序贝尔实验中交换测量设置时，不同置换对称性（置换不变态与非对称态）对非局域性统计结果的影响，并推导两种操作后果：一是通过联合违反统计检测Alice和Bob测量选择之间的相关性；二是在有限测量池的贝尔测试中分析不对称最大纠缠态提高非局域性观测概率的机制。

Result: 置换不变态在两个实验中产生相同的非局域性结果；非对称态可能在一个实验中违反局域实在论而在另一个中不违反。这一特性可用于检测测量选择的相关性，且不对称最大纠缠态在有限测量池中能显著提高观测非局域性的概率而不需要额外资源。

Conclusion: 置换不对称性是随机化贝尔实验中的一个有用特征，对称性在量子非局域性中扮演了新的角色。

Abstract: All maximally entangled two-qubit states violate local realism with the same probability under uniformly random projective measurements, yet they need not behave identically in randomized Bell tests. We show that when measurement settings are exchanged between the parties in sequential Bell experiments, permutation symmetry of the shared state determines the statistical relation between the two realizations. Permutationally invariant states yield identical nonlocality outcomes in both experiments, whereas asymmetric states can violate local realism in one realization but not in the other. This distinction leads to two operational consequences. First, it enables the detection of correlations between the measurement choices of Alice and Bob through the joint violation statistics. Second, in Bell tests with finite measurement pools, asymmetric maximally entangled states can significantly increase the probability of observing nonlocality without requiring additional resources. Our results identify permutation asymmetry as a useful feature in randomized Bell experiments and highlight a new role of symmetry in quantum nonlocality.

</details>


### [102] [Optimal observables for quantum-enhanced sensing and applications in a Floquet time crystal sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26248)
*M. A. Manya,Andrei Tsypilnikov,Fernando Iemini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Floquet时间晶体（FTC）传感器中，如何用更简单的可观测量（如自旋磁化或宇称算符）近似替代对称对数导数（SLD）算符，从而在矩方法（MoM）测量协议中实现接近量子Fisher信息（QFI）界的最优计量，并在核磁共振系统中通过仿真验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称对数导数（SLD）算符虽可饱和量子Fisher信息界，但其结构复杂且通常非局域，难以直接实验实现。因此，需要寻找能够近似SLD的简单可观测量，以在实际量子计量中实现近最优灵敏度。

Method: 首先证明SLD作为厄米可观测量在矩方法中可饱和QFI界；其次在Floquet时间晶体传感模型中分析SLD结构，发现其可通过裸自旋磁化或宇称可观测量近似；最后在核磁共振系统中采用实验可行的参数进行性能仿真。

Result: 在FTC传感模型中，针对不同初始态制备，SLD可被自旋磁化或宇称算符等简单可观测量有效近似；基于实验参数的核磁共振系统仿真验证了该近似的可行性，表明可保持量子增强的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 本文为FTC传感器中实现近最优计量提供了实用途径，即用简单可观测量替代不可实现的SLD算符，仍能保留量子增强的灵敏度。

Abstract: In this work, we discuss how to determine and implement feasible optimal observables for a metrology protocol that saturates the quantum Fisher information (QFI) bound. In particular, we focus our study on a simple protocol, namely the method of moments (MoM). We first demonstrate that the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) operator, a Hermitian observable, once implemented in the MoM, saturates the QFI bound. However, the SLD is generally too complex and typically non-local, rendering its direct experimental realization unfeasible. To overcome this limitation, we explore its structure in a specific sensing model - a Floquet time crystal (FTC) acting as an ac field sensor - and show that the SLD can be approximated by substantially simpler observables, such as the bare spin magnetization or a parity observable, for different relevant initial state preparations. We further corroborate our theoretical predictions in a nuclear magnetic resonance system operating as an FTC sensor, employing experimentally motivated parameters to simulate its performance in a state-of-the-art implementation. In general, our results establish a practical route toward near-optimal metrology in FTC sensors, where the inaccessible SLD operator can be replaced by simpler observables while retaining quantum-enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [103] [A Survey of Quantum Programming Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26254)
*Quan Do,Hersh Gupta,Xiyuan Cao,Aarav Pabla,Pranav Singamsetty,Evan O'Grady,Keli Huang,Jens Palsberg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个语言分类框架，并以此调查了十种流行的量子编程语言，通过概念和实验比较，列出了未来语言设计面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算取得多项突破，越来越接近在某些问题上展示对经典计算的指数级优势，程序员需要高级、通用、可执行的编程语言来清晰有效地表达量子解决方案，因此有必要对现有量子编程语言进行系统分类和比较分析。

Method: 提出了一个语言分类框架，并使用该框架对十种流行的量子编程语言进行调查，包括概念比较和实验比较。

Result: 得出了概念和实验两方面的比较结果，并据此列出未来语言设计所面临的挑战清单。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子编程语言的分类和评估提供了系统性框架，明确了当前语言设计中的不足以及未来需要解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Quantum computing has seen multiple recent breakthroughs and is getting closer to demonstrations of an exponential advantage over classical computing for certain problems. Programmers will require high-level, general-purpose, executable programming languages to express quantum solutions clearly and effectively, and the field has already produced a wide variety of such languages. This paper presents a language classification framework and uses it to survey ten popular quantum programming languages. The findings include conceptual and experimental comparisons that result in a list of challenges for future language design.

</details>


### [104] [Complex frequency-dependent quadrature squeezing in semiconductor lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26266)
*Daniele Nello,Giuseppe Patera,Lorenzo Columbo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于全量子Langevin方法，系统研究了量子阱激光器中的正交压缩，首次揭示了半导体激光器中的频率相关压缩和复杂/隐藏压缩，并分析了线宽增强因子（α因子）的作用，确立了半导体激光器作为非经典光源的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索半导体激光器中是否存在频率相关的正交压缩以及复杂/隐藏压缩现象，并分析线宽增强因子（α因子）在这些现象中的作用。

Method: 采用全量子Langevin方法，计算激光场频率分辨的压缩图谱，识别最优压缩曲线。

Result: 首次发现半导体激光器中存在频率相关的压缩和复杂/隐藏压缩；线宽增强因子（α因子）对这些特征的出现起关键作用。

Conclusion: 半导体激光器可作为产生非经典光的平台，为量子通信和量子传感应用开辟了新视角。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of quadrature squeezing in a quantum well laser based on a fully quantum Langevin approach. We compute the frequency-resolved squeezing map of the laser field and identify optimal squeezing curves, revealing, for the first time to our knowledge, both frequency-dependent squeezing and complex or hidden squeezing in a semiconductor laser. We further analyse the role of the linewidth enhancement factor (alpha factor) in the emergence of these features. Our results establish semiconductor lasers as a platform for the generation of non-classical light and open new perspectives for their application in quantum communication and sensing.

</details>


### [105] [Mechanisms governing photon-pair generation and emission directionality in quantum metasurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26303)
*Alberto Paniate,Ivano Ruo-Berchera,Francesco Monticone*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了影响超表面自发参量下转换产生光子对的效率、发射方向性和收集效率的关键物理机制，揭示了不同泵浦配置和测量几何结构的影响，并提出了通过衬底厚度和多层配置优化光子对源的设计指导原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超表面虽在光子对生成方面具有潜力，但由于支配光子对生成效率和发射方向性的物理机制尚未完全理解，设计常依赖试错法，限制了其潜力发挥。

Method: 通过理论分析不同泵浦配置和测量几何结构对生成效率、发射方向性和收集效率的影响，建立理论框架，并探索衬底厚度和多层配置作为额外设计自由度。

Result: 建立的理论框架能够解释现有实验结果，提供了超表面设计和实验配置选择的一般性指导原则；发现衬底厚度和多层配置可有效增强生成效率并控制发射方向性。

Conclusion: 衬底厚度和多层配置是量子超表面设计的新自由度，为基于超表面的光子对源的优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: Metasurfaces are emerging as a promising platform for photon-pair generation through spontaneous parametric down-conversion, thanks to their compactness, integrability, and intrinsic multifunctionality, which enables the engineering of complex quantum states. However, their full potential remains only partially exploited because the physical mechanisms governing key properties of the generated photon pairs, such as generation efficiency and emission directionality, are not yet fully understood. As a result, metasurface designs and experimental configurations are often optimized through trial-and-error procedures. Here, we theoretically investigate the main mechanisms that control photon-pair generation and detection by studying how different pump configurations and measurement geometries affect the generation efficiency, emission directionality, and collection efficiency of the emitted photon pairs. This framework allows us to interpret existing experimental results and to provide general guidelines for the design of metasurfaces and the choice of experimental configurations in future experiments. Finally, we show that substrate thickness and multilayer configurations represent additional degrees of freedom for quantum metasurface design and can be engineered to enhance the generation efficiency and control the emission directionality, providing a new route for the optimization of photon-pair sources based on metasurfaces.

</details>


### [106] [Tailor Made Embeddings for Quantum Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26312)
*Aldo Lamarre,Dominik Šafránek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子变分自编码器框架，将经典自编码器范式扩展到量子机器学习，实现了高维经典数据（如ImageNet）到13量子比特量子表示的压缩与重构，在MNIST任务上达到98.5%验证准确率，并能在真实IBM量子硬件上稳定运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典自编码器通过解决维度灾难、实现原则性权重初始化和学习紧凑结构化表示，彻底改变了机器学习。本文旨在将这一成功范式扩展到量子机器学习领域，解决现有量子嵌入方法（如振幅嵌入需全量子态层析、角度嵌入依赖电路反演）的可重构性差和测量开销大的问题。

Method: 提出量子变分自编码器（QVAE）框架，学习任务特定的经典数据量子嵌入。编码器将高维经典数据压缩为少量量子比特（如13量子比特）的量子态表示，解码器学习从该量子表示中重构原始数据。仅需多项式数量的测量即可完成数据重构，而非指数级。

Result: 在MNIST（3 vs 5）二分类任务上达到98.5%验证准确率，仅比经典神经网络基线（99.7%）低1.2个百分点，比朴素振幅嵌入方法高出30个百分点以上。高维数据集ImageNet可压缩至13量子比特表示并可重构。在真实IBM量子硬件上验证了嵌入的稳定性和可重构性。

Conclusion: 量子变分自编码器框架成功将经典自编码器的优势引入量子机器学习，在有限量子资源下实现了高维数据的紧凑量子表示与高效重构，且对真实量子噪声具有鲁棒性，为量子机器学习中的表示学习提供了实用化路径。

Abstract: Autoencoders transformed classical machine learning by solving the curse of dimensionality, enabling principled weight initialization and learning compact, structured representations. In this work, we extend this paradigm to quantum machine learning by introducing a variational autoencoder framework that learns task-specific quantum embeddings of classical data. We demonstrate that high-dimensional datasets, including ImageNet, can be compressed into a 13-qubit quantum representation while remaining reconstructable through a learned decoder. On MNIST (3 vs 5), our approach achieves 98.5% validation accuracy using a circuit-centric quantum classifier, within 1.2 percentage points of a classical neural network baseline (99.7%) and more than 30 percentage points above a naive amplitude-embedding approach. Unlike amplitude embeddings, which require full quantum state tomography for recovery, or angle embeddings, which generally rely on circuit inversion under restrictive assumptions, the proposed framework reconstructs the original data from only a polynomial number of measurements. The framework was further validated on IBM quantum hardware, confirming that the learned embeddings remain stable and reconstructable under real device noise.

</details>


### [107] [Improving device-independent quantum key distribution protocols through multiple routed Bell tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26329)
*Sujan Vijayaraj,Mauro Paternostro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多源和多测量设备的设备无关量子密钥分发(DI-QKD)协议，通过路由贝尔测试和纠缠交换实现长距离安全通信，并利用条件局域测试的一致性检查将关键探测效率提高了约4-12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DI-QKD提供最小假设下的安全性，但实际实现面临距离限制，因为远距离贝尔态测量仍需要接近理想的局域测试违规。现有方案难以同时兼顾长距离和高探测效率。

Method: 提出基于多源和多测量设备的DI-QKD协议，每轮在随机选择的局域设备上执行路由测试；即使成功实现BSM投影，仍检查局域贝尔测试的违规情况；要求条件测试与整体测试一致；将路由概念扩展到维度见证，实现半设备无关扩展。

Result: 在高可见度下，关键探测效率提高了约4-12%；实现了长距离DI-QKD，支持高效无漏洞路由设置和多个（可能不完美的）局域测试。

Conclusion: 该方法通过条件局域测试与全局测试的一致性约束，在保持安全性的同时显著提升了探测效率，为实用化长距离DI-QKD提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Device-independent quantum key distribution (DI-QKD) offers security with the smallest possible set of assumptions about the experimental setup. The challenge posed by its implementation could be tackled using routed Bell tests with entanglement swapping, or distant Bell state measurement (BSM) units. However, practical distances still require local tests with close-to-ideal violations. We propose a DI-QKD protocol based on multiple sources and measurement devices where, in each round, routed tests are performed on randomly selected local devices. The violation of local Bell tests is checked even when a successful BSM projection is achieved. By requiring that such conditional tests remain consistent with the overall one, we achieve improvements in the critical detection efficiencies of about $4-12\%$ for high visibilities. Our approach enables long-distance DI-QKD, with access to highly efficient loophole-free routing setups, and multiple local tests (possibly imperfect) with very high local detection efficiencies. Finally, we extend the concept of routing to dimension witnesses, where qubit-bounded sources send states to the BSM. This can be seen as a semi-device-independent extension of the aforementioned protocol.

</details>


### [108] [Equivalence of non-local computation tasks beyond Clifford operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26354)
*Andreas Bluhm,Simon Höfer,Alex May,Florian Speelman,Philip Verduyn Lunel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非局部量子计算（NLQC）任务间的归约关系，重点关注量子位置验证中最可行的方案（大经典输入+固定大小量子输入）。发现许多看似不同的NLQC任务之间存在归约关系，例如基于经典控制重向量子系统的最简协议可用于实现任意基下的受控单量子比特测量、受控Clifford酉操作以及形如U=C₁DC₀的任意酉操作（D为对角酉，C₀,C₁为Clifford电路）。这表明许多可行的位置验证方案具有相同的纠缠代价渐近标度，从而具有相似的安全等级。技术方面借鉴了门隐形传态和基于测量的量子计算等思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继续研究NLQC任务间的归约关系，揭示不同NLQC任务的相对难度，并探究量子位置验证中最可行方案的内在联系与安全等级。

Method: 采用归约方法，通过门隐形传态和基于测量的量子计算等技术，建立不同NLQC任务间的实现关系。

Result: 发现最简单NLQC协议可归约出多种复杂操作（受控测量、受控Clifford酉、形如U=C₁DC₀的酉操作），证明许多位置验证方案具有相同的纠缠代价渐近标度。

Conclusion: 许多可行的位置验证方案具有相似的纠缠代价渐近标度和安全等级；所采用的归约技术（门隐形传态、基于测量的量子计算等）具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) studies how two collaborating players can implement channels on distributed systems using a single simultaneous round of quantum communication and shared entanglement. NLQC has applications in diverse areas, ranging from quantum position-verification to quantum gravity. Recently, it has been realized that the relationships among families of NLQC tasks are highly structured: many seemingly distinct tasks are related by reductions, wherein implementations of one task can be used to efficiently implement a second task. This is analogous to the notion of reduction in complexity theory, and reveals the relative hardness of NLQC tasks. In this work we continue the study of reductions among NLQC tasks. We focus on NLQC examples of the greatest interest in quantum position-verification; in particular examples involving large classical inputs and fixed-size quantum inputs, since these constitute the most feasible protocols for position-verification schemes. Within this setting, we find many new relationships among NLQC tasks. For instance, protocols for the simplest example of redirecting a quantum system based on a classical control imply protocols for controlled single qubit measurements in arbitrary bases, the controlled application of any Clifford unitary, and even the controlled application of any unitary of the form $U=C_1DC_0$ with $D$ an arbitrary diagonal unitary and $C_0, C_1$ Clifford circuits. This implies that many feasible position-verification schemes have the same asymptotic scaling for their entanglement cost, and hence a similar level of security. Our techniques rely on ideas from gate teleportation and measurement based quantum computation, among other areas, bringing several new strategies into NLQC which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [109] [Algebraic structures of the Lindblad equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26477)
*Leonel Bixano,Guillermo López-Alvarez,Victor Alberto Cruz-Barriguete,V. G. Ibarra-Sierra,José Luis Cardoso,Juan Carlos Sandoval-Santana,Alejandro Kunold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限维开放量子系统中Lindblad方程背后的代数结构，通过引入Liouville超算符的算符表示，证明动力学可由与具体物理模型无关的闭Hermitian算符代数描述，并给出递归构造方法和高效实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示Lindblad方程所描述的耗散动力学相较于纯幺正演化需要更丰富的代数结构，并为这种额外复杂性提供清晰刻画。

Method: 引入Liouville超算符的算符表示，建立闭Hermitian算符代数；推导动力学映射参数化及控制演化的微分方程；给出可高效构建任意维度系统代数的递归关系。

Result: 代数基是通用的，所有模型相关信息仅通过一组系数进入，显著降低了构建Liouville超算符的计算成本；提供了Mathematica示例实现。

Conclusion: 该框架既为有限维Lindblad动力学提供了通用的数学描述，也为高效的解析和数值实现奠定了实用基础。

Abstract: We investigate the algebraic structure underlying the Lindblad equation for finite-dimensional open quantum systems. By introducing a suitable operator representation of the Liouville superoperator, we show that the dynamics can be formulated in terms of a closed algebra of Hermitian operators that is independent of the particular physical model. This formulation reveals that dissipative dynamics requires a substantially richer algebraic structure than purely unitary evolution, thereby providing a clear characterization of the additional complexity introduced by the Lindbladian. The resulting framework naturally leads to parametrizations of the dynamical map and to differential equations governing its evolution. We further derive recursion relations that enable the efficient construction of the algebra for systems of increasing dimension. Because the algebraic basis is universal, while all model-dependent information enters through a single set of coefficients, the proposed approach significantly reduces the computational cost of constructing the Liouville superoperator compared with direct methods. To facilitate the implementation of the method, we provide a Mathematica notebook containing a one-qubit example that can be systematically extended to an arbitrary number of qubits. The proposed framework therefore provides both a general mathematical description of finite-dimensional Lindblad dynamics and a practical foundation for efficient analytical and numerical implementations.

</details>


### [110] [Non-Hermitian Bloch Oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26480)
*Yanyan He,Tomoki Ozawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了非厄米布洛赫振荡的理论框架，研究了直流驱动下非厄米晶格中的波包动力学，发现了非互易非光滑布洛赫振荡和周期性的Goos-Hänchen位移等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立非厄米系统中布洛赫振荡的通用理论框架，揭示非厄米性对波包动力学的影响。

Method: 通过推导一维非厄米晶格中直流驱动下波包的动量、质心和群速度的运动方程，分析非厄米性导致的异常群速度，并研究有限尺寸效应和单向跳跃边界条件。

Result: 发现了非互易非光滑布洛赫振荡（群速度周期性跳跃），以及在开边界单向跳跃晶格中的周期性Goos-Hänchen位移和沿零跳跃方向的异常波传播。

Conclusion: 非厄米性显著改变了布洛赫振荡的行为，产生了传统厄米系统中不存在的奇特动力学现象。

Abstract: We establish a general framework for non-Hermitian Bloch oscillations by investigating the wave-packet dynamics in one-dimensional non-Hermitian lattices driven by a dc force. The equations of motion for the momentum, center of mass, and group velocity of a wave packet are derived, where an anomalous group velocity due to the non-Hermiticity is identified. We show that nonreciprocal non-smooth Bloch oscillations, characterized by periodic jumps in group velocity, can emerge, and we analyze the role of finite-size effects. In non-Hermitian lattices with unidirectional hopping under open boundary conditions, we further uncover the emergence of periodic temporal Goos--Hänchen shifts together with an anomalous wave propagation along the direction of vanishing hopping.

</details>


### [111] [Scattering theory for cavity-assisted spin-motion-photon interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26542)
*Seigo Kikura,Aruku Senoo,Akihisa Goban,Shinichi Sunami*

Main category: quant-ph
