TL;DR: 本文为腔辅助光子散射（CAPS）建立了包含原子运动自由度的完整解析框架，通过将散射理论扩展到运动自由度，得到了统一的基于算子的输入-输出关系，适用于各种腔几何结构、自旋相关陷阱势及非全同多自旋系统。作为应用示例，揭示了原子运动对CAPS原子-光子门的影响机制，并识别了抑制运动诱导误差的参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAPS是实现静止量子比特与飞行光子强相互作用的关键机制，但现有理论在共振耦合区间缺乏对自旋-运动-光子耦合的解析描述。随着运动量子态成为量子信息处理中的重要自由度及误差通道，亟需建立包含运动自由度的完整CAPS理论框架。

Method: 通过将散射理论扩展到包含运动自由度，发展了一个完整的解析框架；推导了紧凑的基于算子的输入-输出关系，统一适用于多种腔几何结构、自旋相关陷阱势以及多个非全同自旋系统。

Result: 建立了包含原子运动的CAPS完整解析理论，阐明了原子运动对CAPS原子-光子门的影响机制，识别了可抑制运动诱导误差的参数区域。

Conclusion: 该框架为CAPS操作中运动误差的抑制以及有意利用运动-光子相互作用提供了理论基础，对混合原子-光子量子信息处理具有重要意义。

Abstract: Cavity-assisted photon scattering (CAPS) is a powerful mechanism for realizing strong interactions between the internal states of stationary qubits and flying photons, underpinning a broad range of hybrid atom-photon protocols including remote entanglement generation and heralded atom-photon gates. Recently, the motional quantum state has emerged as an important building block for quantum information processing with atomic qubits, both as a coherently controllable degree of freedom and as a fundamental error channel through undesired spin-motion coupling. For the resonant-coupling regime of cavity quantum electrodynamics relevant to CAPS operations, however, the analytical formulation of spin-motion-photon coupling has so far remained elusive. Here, we develop a complete analytical framework for CAPS that incorporates the coherent interaction between atomic motion and a reflected photon by extending scattering theory to include the motional degree of freedom. The resulting compact operator-based input-output relation applies uniformly across various cavity geometries, spin-dependent trapping potentials, and nonidentical multiple spins. As an exemplary application, we use the framework to elucidate how atomic motion affects CAPS-based atom-photon gates, identifying the parameter regimes that suppress motion-induced errors. Our framework provides a theoretical foundation both for mitigating motional errors in CAPS operations and for deliberately exploiting motion-photon interaction at the atom-photon interface.

</details>


### [112] [Graph Structures for Local Distinguishability of Quantum Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26558)
*Sooyeong Kim,David W. Kribs,Michael Nathanson,Rajesh Pereira,Sarah Plosker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用图论区分量子乘积态集合，将先前的一向LOCC工作扩展至双向LOCC，分析了对应可区分态的二部图性质，给出了基本闭包性质、可区分与不可区分的图类，并附有示例和展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将图论方法从单向LOCC区分量子乘积态扩展到更一般的双向LOCC情形，填补该方向上的理论空白。

Method: 通过图论建模，定义并分析对应二部可区分图的集合，推导闭包性质，识别保证局部可区分的图类以及反例。

Result: 得出可区分图集的基本闭包性质；发现若干保证局部可区分性的图类以及一些不可区分的图类。

Conclusion: 图论为分析双向LOCC下的量子乘积态可区分性提供了有效工具，但仍有大量开放问题待探索。

Abstract: We consider the problem of distinguishing sets of quantum product states with local operations and classical communication (LOCC). Recent work has used graph theory to identify sets of product states distinguishable with one-way LOCC. We extend these efforts to full two-way LOCC, with the first significant analysis of the set of graphs corresponding to bipartite product states that can be distinguished with two-way protocols after finitely many steps. We derive basic closure properties of the set of distinguishable graphs and identify some classes of graphs that guarantee local distinguishability and some graphs that do not. We also include several examples and forward-looking comments.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum Fast-Forwarding Beyond Reversibility: The $α$-Perturbed $n$-Cycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26584)
*Avah Banerjee,Asim Sharma,Sooraj Sooman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 量子快速前向量子行走（QFF）在可逆马尔可夫链中可通过切比雪夫多项式精确实现，但本文证明对于α扰动的n循环非可逆马尔可夫链，由于特征值超出[-1,1]区间，精确切比雪夫QFF无法直接推广；然而通过截断切比雪夫和LCU技术，可以在有限时间内以τ=O(|α|t+√(t log(t/η)))的复杂度近似演化P_α^t，仅在|α|=O(t^{-1/2})的微扰恢复可逆的O(√t)加速，揭示了不可逆性如何削弱量子加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子快速前向（QFF）框架能否从可逆马尔可夫链扩展到非可逆动力学，研究非可逆性对量子加速的影响。

Method: 采用α扰动的n循环马尔可夫链作为非可逆模型，通过谱分析证明特征值超出[-1,1]导致精确切比雪夫QFF受阻，随后利用截断切比雪夫多项式和线性组合酉算子（LCU）技术实现有限时间近似。

Result: 非可逆情况下精确切比雪夫QFF无法直接推广；近似方案复杂度为τ=O(|α|t+√(t log(t/η)))，仅在|α|=O(t^{-1/2})微扰区域恢复O(√t)加速。

Conclusion: 识别出近乎可逆的微扰区域是QFF幸存的条件，量化了不可逆性对量子加速的退化作用。

Abstract: Quantum fast-forwarding (QFF) is usually formulated for reversible Markov chains, where the projected quantum walk evolution is exactly governed by Chebyshev polynomials of a Hermitian discriminant matrix. We study whether this framework can be extended to nonreversible dynamics for an $α$-perturbed $n$-cycle Markov chain, which preserves circulant structure while introducing controlled irreversibility.
  We show that the nonreversible case has a fundamental obstruction: for $α\neq 0$, the eigenvalues of $P_α$ leave the interval $[-1,1]$, so $T_m(P_α)$ is not uniformly bounded and cannot arise as an exact unitary compression for all times. Thus, exact Chebyshev-based QFF does not extend directly beyond reversibility.
  Nevertheless, we obtain a finite-time approximation result using truncated Chebyshev and LCU techniques. The evolution $P_α^t$ can be approximated with degree $τ=O\left(|α|t+\sqrt{t\log(t/η)}\right),$ which recovers the reversible $O(\sqrt t)$ behavior only in the perturbative regime $|α|=O(t^{-1/2})$. This identifies a nearly reversible regime where QFF survives perturbatively and quantifies how irreversibility degrades the speedup.

</details>


### [114] [Non-Hermiticity of an anomalous superradiant phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26770)
*Jun-Ling Wang,You-Qi Lu,Qing-Hu Chen,Yu-Yu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种厄米压缩-迪克模型，通过纯厄米系统实现非厄米物理，包括有效PT对称性、异常超辐射相、奇异点动力学以及非互易量子放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非厄米物理机制通常依赖耗散驱动，作者希望探索一种无需耗散、仅通过厄米系统（压缩和自旋耦合）实现非厄米现象的新途径，从而简化实验实现并揭示新的物理机制。

Method: 构建一个厄米压缩-迪克模型（Hermitian squeezing-Dicke model），利用二次玻色子系统中的压缩效应和自旋-光耦合，在厄米框架下等效实现非厄米哈密顿量。通过分析PT对称性的出现条件、激发谱的复能级结构以及人工磁场下的动力学行为，研究系统的非厄米特性。

Result: 1) 有效PT对称性源于压缩而非耗散，增益和损耗本征模式可互换；2) 发现强自旋压缩下的异常超辐射相，该相具有自发破缺的PT对称性（超越Z₂对称性），其激发谱为复数；3) 系统在奇异点处经历从异常超辐射相到常规超辐射相的动力学相变；4) 结合人工磁场与破缺厄米性，实现非互易动力学和单向增强传输（量子放大）。

Conclusion: 该厄米光-物质系统为研究非厄米物理和非互易量子放大提供了一种替代方案，无需实际引入增益/损耗介质，拓展了非厄米物理在量子光学和量子信息处理中的应用前景。

Abstract: We counterintuitively present a Hermitian squeezing-Dicke model as a minimal setting for non-Hermitian physics in many-body light-matter systems. It enables the realization of a non-Hermitian Hamiltonian of interest using a Hermitian quadratic bosonic system. Unlike previous dissipation-driven non-Hermitian mechanisms, effective parity-time ($\mathcal{PT}$) symmetry arises purely from squeezing and exchanges gainy and lossy eigenmodes. We identify non-Hermiticity of an anomalous superradiant phase for strong spins squeezing, exhibiting spontaneous breaking of the unique $\mathcal{PT}$ symmetry beyond $Z_2$ symmetries. Such exotic phase exhibits a complex excitation spectrum and undergoes a dynamical phase transition to a conventional superradiant phase at an exceptional point. An artificial magnetic field combined with the broken Hermiticity yields nonreciprocal dynamics with striking quantum amplification, exhibiting unidirectional enhanced transmission. Our Hermitian light-matter system offers an alternative pathway to exotic non-Hermitian physics and nonreciprocal quantum amplification.

</details>


### [115] [Nonadiabatic Holonomic Single-Qubit Gates in Non-Hermitian Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26798)
*Wei Li,Yue Zhang,Yu Kun Chen,Jia Yao Liang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非绝热全同量子计算方案，通过在无跳变区间的非厄米三能级系统中设计复值脉冲，利用双正交框架实现任意单量子比特全同门操作。该方案将系统中所有本征态的衰减和退相干直接纳入脉冲设计，使得耗散不会降低无跳变门保真度，克服了现有非厄米处理方法中忽略指数前因子或仅在绝热设置中考虑辅助激发态耗散的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全同量子计算虽能实现鲁棒的量子门，但退相干仍是实际实现中的核心障碍。现有非厄米处理方法要么忽略整体指数前因子，要么仅在绝热条件下考虑辅助激发态的耗散，无法有效处理所有本征态的衰减和退相干问题。

Method: 开发了一个针对驱动三能级系统的非绝热全同方案，系统处于由有效非厄米哈密顿量描述的无跳变区间。在双正交框架下，设计定制的复值脉冲，使得尽管存在非幺正动力学，计算子空间演化在最终时刻精确闭合，实现任意单量子比特全同门，无需在正交补空间中进行循环演化。

Result: 该方案将衰减和退相干直接纳入脉冲设计，因此耗散不会降低无跳变门保真度，实现了对现有非厄米处理方法的改进。

Conclusion: 该非绝热全同方案通过将耗散直接融入脉冲设计，有效消除了退相干对无跳变门保真度的负面影响，为鲁棒量子门实现提供了新途径。

Abstract: Holonomic quantum computation offers a promising route to robust quantum gates, but decoherence remains a central obstacle in realistic implementations. Here we develop a nonadiabatic holonomic scheme for a driven three-level system in the no-jump regime described by an effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian. Within a biorthogonal framework, tailored complex pulses enforce exact closure of the computational-subspace evolution at the final time despite the underlying nonunitary dynamics, enabling arbitrary single-qubit holonomic gates without requiring cyclic evolution in its orthogonal complement. In contrast to existing non-Hermitian treatments, which either neglect the overall exponential prefactor or, in adiabatic settings, include dissipation only on the auxiliary excited level, our scheme incorporates decay and dephasing of all bare eigenstates directly into the pulse design, so that dissipation does not reduce the no-jump gate fidelity.

</details>


### [116] [Dissipation-Induced Deviations from Kibble-Zurek Scaling in Non-Hermitian Quantum Annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26870)
*H. Najafzadeh,S. Sadeghizade,R. Jafari,A. Langari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非厄米横场Ising模型中的量子退火问题，发现缺陷产生不再局限于能隙闭合点附近的模式，而是涉及广泛动量区间的贡献。根据耗散强度不同，缺陷密度表现出标准Kibble-Zurek标度、反Kibble-Zurek行为以及比KZ预测更快的抑制三种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Kibble-Zurek机制主要基于厄米系统，在非厄米系统中量子退火的缺陷产生机制尚不清楚，需要重新审视。

Method: 通过解析和数值两种方法，研究非厄米横场Ising模型中的量子退火问题，计算本征跃迁概率和缺陷密度。

Result: 发现缺陷密度依赖于耗散强度，呈现三种标度行为：标准KZ标度、反KZ行为（缺陷密度随退火时间增加而增加）以及比KZ预测更快的抑制。快速抑制源于跨越所有允许模式的激发概率消失，反KZ行为源于耗散在远离能隙闭合点的广泛模式中诱导的额外激发。

Conclusion: 非厄米动力学中缺陷产生涉及广泛动量区间，不同于厄米情形；非厄米性可导致偏离标准KZ标度的新奇行为。

Abstract: We revisit the quantum annealing problem in the non-Hermitian transverse-field Ising model. We determine, both analytically and numerically, the intrinsic transition probabilities and the resulting defect density. Our results reveal that, unlike the Hermitian case where defect production is dominated by modes near the gap-closing point, the non-Hermitian dynamics involve significant contributions from broad momentum sectors. We find that, depending on the dissipation strength, the defect density exhibits standard Kibble-Zurek scaling, anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior, and a suppression faster than the Kibble-Zurek prediction. We demonstrate that these deviations from the standard Kibble-Zurek scaling can be understood in terms of the underlying excitation probabilities. Specifically, the fast decay of the defect density originates from a vanishing excitation probability spanning a range of annealing times across all allowed modes, even at the gap-closing points. In contrast, the anti-Kibble-Zurek behavior arises from supplementary excitations facilitated by dissipation over a broad range of allowed modes, particularly those situated away from the gap-closing region.

</details>


### [117] [Scalable Message-Passing Quantum Graph Neural Networks in the Weisfeiler-Leman Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26873)
*Snehal Raj,Brian Coyle,Léo Monbroussou,André J. Ferreira-Martins,Renato M. S. Farias,Elham Kashefi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子图神经网络框架，能够执行消息传递、保持排列等变性，并可在Weisfeiler-Lehman层次结构的任意级别运行。通过在小图上预训练缓解训练问题，并在多达56量子比特的大规模模拟中验证了其在图区分、分子性质预测和旅行商问题上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子图神经网络与经典消息传递机制联系有限，且缺乏性能或可扩展性保证；变分量子电路的训练困难是广泛应用的瓶颈。

Method: 构建量子图神经网络实现消息传递和排列等变，可设定在Weisfeiler-Lehman层次结构的指定级别；采用小图预训练策略缓解训练问题；图增长时读出成本保持低位。

Result: 在多达56量子比特的大规模模拟中验证，涵盖普通消息传递无法区分的合成图、分子性质预测和旅行商问题三个数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架为近期量子算法提供了理论保证和实际可扩展性，将图学习原理引入量子电路设计。

Abstract: Graphs provide a natural language for relational data in chemistry, biology and optimisation. Graph neural networks (GNNs) have driven much of the recent progress in learning from such data through message passing, a single primitive that generalises convolution and attention. Quantum counterparts have been proposed, but with limited connection to message passing and few guarantees on performance or scalability. More broadly, the trainability of variational quantum circuits is a recognised bottleneck for their wide applicability, and pre-training has emerged as one way to address it. Yet for a quantum model to be useful, it must offer expressivity guarantees along with demonstrable scalability. Here we show how a quantum graph neural network can be built to perform message passing, to be permutation equivariant, and to sit at a chosen level of the Weisfeiler-Leman hierarchy, the standard measure of how finely a model can tell graphs apart. We show that, as for classical GNNs, the training can be done first on small graph instances, allowing for a pre-training that can mitigate usual training issues, and its output can be read out at a cost that stays low as the graph grows. We validate the framework in large-scale simulations of up to 56 qubits across three datasets, on synthetic graphs that ordinary message passing cannot separate, on molecular property prediction, and on the travelling salesperson problem. Our framework opens a path for near-term quantum algorithms with theoretical guarantees and practical scalability, bringing the principles of graph learning into quantum circuit design.

</details>


### [118] [Shallow Quantum Circuits for Deep Chemistry via Valence Bond Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26882)
*Francisco Javier del Arco Santos,Jakob S. Kottmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合混合费米子-玻色子编码与量子价键理论的结构化方法，为大型分子系统直接构建量子电路，突破了变分量子本征求解器在化学相关系统中的可模拟性壁垒，实现了优于活性空间方法的近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子化学计算受限于硬件噪声和指数级瓶颈，主要集中在较小的活性空间。长期来看，需要为更大系统提供可靠的近似方法，但目前缺乏能产生总系统统一量子电路的一致方法。

Method: 将混合费米子-玻色子编码与量子价键理论的结构化方法相结合，直接构建适用于较大分子系统的量子电路。

Result: 该方法将变分量子本征求解器的可模拟性推向了化学相关系统；电路设计优于活性空间对应方法，且与精确解达到良好近似。

Conclusion: 该组合方法为大型分子系统的量子化学计算提供了一种有效且一致的电路构建方案，有望成为投影算法（如量子相位估计）的初始态及动力学性质评估的有力工具。

Abstract: Quantum chemistry is one of the major potential applications in quantum computation. Currently there is a considerable focus on relatively small active spaces as a consequence of hardware noise and exponential bottlenecks in simulations. In the long run, there will be an increasing demand in reliable approximations for larger systems -- both, as initial states for projective algorithms like the quantum phase estimation or for the evaluation of dynamical properties. While numerous approaches to select active spaces and extrapolate basis set accuracy exist, there is currently no consistent approach that results in a single quantum circuit for the total system. In this work, we combine hybrid Fermionic-Bosonic encodings with the structured approach of Quantum Valence Bond Theory to directly construct quantum circuits for comparably large molecular systems. With this approach we are able to push simulability barrier of variational quantum eigensolvers towards chemically relevant systems and demonstrate circuit designs that outperform active space counterparts and achieve good approximations with respect to the exact solutions.

</details>


### [119] [An ultralow-loss integrated photonic platform for discrete-variable quantum information processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26910)
*Yi-Han Luo,Ruiyang Chen,Zeying Zhong,Sanli Huang,Sicheng Zeng,Zhenyuan Shang,Yue Hu,Zhen Chen,Yuan Chen,Xue Bai,Junqiu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种基于超低损耗氮化硅（Si₃N₄）的单片集成光子平台，通过集成窄带光子对源、低损耗量子比特融合电路和可重构态分析干涉仪，克服了光子集成电路中的速率-损耗壁垒。在片上实现了保真度0.9875(3)的EPR态制备、0.990(6)的Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉可见度，以及保真度0.943(8)、四重计数率27 Hz的四光子GHZ态合成，性能比以往硅光实现高出两个数量级以上，为大规模可部署量子信息处理器提供了可制造的CMOS兼容平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路虽在离散变量量子信息处理中取得多项突破，但光学损耗随电路深度和复杂度快速累积，导致多光子产生概率指数下降，严重制约了平台从原理验证向规模化应用的发展，亟需克服这一系统级速率-损耗瓶颈。

Method: 设计并制备了基于超低损耗氮化硅（Si₃N₄）的单片集成光子平台，集成窄带光子对源、低损耗量子比特融合电路和可重构态分析干涉仪，通过片上EPR态制备与融合实现四光子GHZ态合成。

Result: EPR态保真度0.9875(3)，光子不可区分性接近1，Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉可见度0.990(6)；四光子GHZ态保真度0.943(8)，四重计数率27 Hz，比此前硅光实现高出两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 超低损耗Si₃N₄集成光子学结合CMOS兼容晶圆制造，确立了其作为可制造、可部署的大规模量子信息处理器的可靠平台地位。

Abstract: Photonic integrated circuits offer a scalable and robust route toward quantum information technologies by consolidating photon sources and linear optical networks onto compact, wafer-manufacturable chips. Although silicon photonics has enabled diverse discrete-variable quantum breakthroughs -- spanning multiphoton entanglement, quantum networking, and photonic qubit fusion for quantum computing -- scaling these platforms beyond proof-of-principle demonstrations remains severely constrained by a critical system-level bottleneck. Optical loss compounds rapidly across photon generation, routing, and state analysis, causing multiphoton generation probabilities to plummet exponentially as circuit depth and complexity grow. Here we overcome this rate-loss barrier by demonstrating a monolithic, ultralow-loss silicon nitride (Si$_3$N$_4$) integrated photonic platform engineered for high-performance discrete-variable quantum information processing. Our architecture seamlessly integrates narrowband photon-pair sources with low-loss qubit-fusion circuits and reconfigurable state-analysis interferometers. The on-chip sources prepare Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen (EPR) states with a fidelity of 0.9875(3) and exhibit near-unity photon indistinguishability, yielding a heralded Hong-Ou-Mandel interference visibility of 0.990(6). By executing on-chip fusion of two EPR states, we synthesize and characterize four-photon Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger states with a record fidelity of 0.943(8) and a fourfold count rate of 27 Hz -- more than two orders of magnitude higher than previous silicon-photonic implementations. Combined with standard CMOS-compatible fabrication on 150-mm-diameter wafers, these results establish ultralow-loss Si$_3$N$_4$ integrated photonics as a definitive, manufacturable platform for deployable, large-scale quantum information processors.

</details>


### [120] [On the simple derivation of the Casimir effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26946)
*Hai-Chau Nguyen,Matthias Kleinmann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文回顾了卡西米尔效应的原始推导，揭示了其中细微但重要的数学细节，旨在提供一个数学上严密的推导完整图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡西米尔效应的标准推导常出现在量子光学入门教材中，但原始论文中存在一些微妙之处值得深入讨论，以呈现数学上更严密的推导过程。

Method: 回归卡西米尔原始论文，细致分析其推导过程中的数学步骤，识别并讨论其中常被忽略的细微差别。

Result: 揭示了原始推导中常被后续教材简化或忽略的数学细节，为卡西米尔效应提供了更完整的数学推导图景。

Conclusion: 卡西米尔效应的标准推导存在细微的数学微妙之处，讨论这些细节有助于获得对该效应数学基础的完整理解。

Abstract: The Casimir effect in its simplest form describes the attraction of two parallel conducting plates at close distance due to the vacuum fluctuation of the electromagnetic field. Its derivation can be found in many introductory works on quantum optics. Here we return to the original paper by Casimir and find subtle nuances in his derivation that are worth discussing to give a complete picture of a mathematically sound derivation of the effect.

</details>


### [121] [Lattice patch structure for fixed-frequency transmon quantum computer with high-fidelity CNOT gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27017)
*Chanpyo Kim,Jeongsoo Kang,Younghun Kwon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对超导transmon处理器中传统qubit-coupler-qubit架构在频率拥挤和表面码映射低效方面的瓶颈，提出了一种将四个固定频率transmon耦合至单个固定频率耦合器的晶格补丁架构，实现了直接映射到表面码格子单元。数值仿真显示CNOT门保真度超过0.98，并可通过虚拟R_z门校准残余相位累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超导transmon处理器采用qubit-coupler-qubit (QCQ)架构，在系统扩展时面临频率拥挤（ spectator qubit碰撞）问题，且难以高效映射到标准表面码上，成为实现大规模容错量子计算的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出一种晶格补丁架构，将四个固定频率transmon耦合至单个固定频率耦合器，直接映射到表面码格子单元（plaquette），采用全固定频率设计消除外部通量噪声影响，并通过虚拟R_z门（软件相位更新）校准残余相位累积。

Result: 多层级数值仿真显示，补丁内六个连接方向上的CNOT门保真度均超过0.98。

Conclusion: 该晶格补丁架构为未来容错量子计算机提供了一种高效且稳健的构建模块。

Abstract: Superconducting transmon processors represent a leading platform for large-scale quantum computing due to their high gate fidelities and scalability. However, conventional qubit-coupler-qubit (QCQ) architectures face critical physical and structural bottlenecks, notably frequency crowding [spectator qubit collisions] during system scaling and inefficient mapping onto the standard surface code.To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel lattice-patch architecture that couples four fixed-frequency transmons to a single fixed-frequency coupler.This design enhances qubit connectivity and maps directly onto the surface-code lattice unit [plaquette], thereby minimizing the compilation overhead associated with logical qubit implementation. Furthermore, utilizing an entirely fixed-frequency design intrinsically eliminates susceptibility to external flux noise, ensuring robust operational stability.Multi-level numerical simulations demonstrate CNOT gate fidelities exceeding 0.98 across all six connectivity directions within the patch. Nevertheless, the complex interaction network of the four-qubit architecture induces unintended residual phase accumulation during cross-resonance driving. This parasitic effect necessitates precise calibration, achievable via virtual $R_z$ gates [software phase updates]. Ultimately, our results establish the lattice-patch architecture as an efficient, robust building block for future fault-tolerant quantum computers.

</details>


### [122] [Witness expansion: A unified framework for analytical and measurable mixed-state resource detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27105)
*Yifan Tang,Chengkai Zhu,Yuzhen Zhang,Jens Eisert,Zi-Wen Liu,Ingo Roth,Otfried Gühne,Xin Wang,Zhenhuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"见证扩展"（witness expansion）统一框架，通过构建基于多项式函数的非线性判据，检测与自由酉群相关的量子资源。该框架统一了相干性、纠缠、量子魔法和费米子非高斯性等多种资源的检测方法，并给出了新的量子魔法态判据以及首个通用的混合态费米子非高斯性分析判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子资源在信息处理任务和基础量子物理问题中日益重要，亟需一种强大且实用的方法统一识别和检测不同类型的量子资源，涵盖纯态与混合态，并能在实验中实现。

Method: 提出"见证扩展"框架，基于目标态的多项式函数构建非线性判据，这些函数可通过多个副本进行实验估计，并在某些物理模型中解析计算。框架适用于与定义明确的自由酉群相关的量子资源。

Result: 成功从框架中自然导出了$l_2$相干性范数、部分转置矩（纠缠检测）、稳定子熵（量子魔法）和费米子反平度（费米子非高斯性）等已知资源检测量；同时给出了新的qubit和qudit魔法态检测判据，以及首个针对费米子高斯态凸包的混合态费米子非高斯性分析判据。

Conclusion: 该框架具有广泛的适用性和概念统一能力，不仅恢复了现有资源检测结构，还显著增强了基于见证的检测能力，为量子资源理论提供了统一的检测方法论。

Abstract: Quantum information science aims to harness different kinds of quantum resources to accomplish specific information-processing tasks. These resources also play an increasingly important role in addressing fundamental questions concerning quantum phases and dynamics. Therefore, developing powerful and practical methods for identifying and detecting quantum resources is of great significance, with applications ranging from benchmarking quantum devices to understanding the fundamental structure of quantum theory. In this work, we propose witness expansion, a unified framework for constructing nonlinear criteria for detecting quantum resources that are associated with a well-defined group of free unitaries. These criteria apply to both pure and mixed quantum states and are based on polynomial functions of the target state, which can be estimated experimentally using multiple copies of the state and evaluated analytically in certain physical models. We show how several well-known resource-detection quantities naturally emerge from our framework, including the $l_2$ norm of coherence, partial-transpose moments for entanglement, stabilizer entropy for nonstabilizerness (quantum magic), and fermionic antiflatness for fermionic non-Gaussianity. Beyond recovering these existing structures, our framework also yields new criteria for detecting qubit and qudit magic states, substantially enhancing witness-based detection capabilities. In addition, it gives, to the best of our knowledge, the first analytical criterion for detecting mixed-state fermionic non-Gaussianity with respect to the convex hull of pure fermionic Gaussian states that remains nontrivial for arbitrary numbers of qubits, demonstrating the broad applicability and conceptual unifying power of the framework.

</details>


### [123] [Efficient foundation decoders for fault-tolerant quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27119)
*Ge Yan,Shanchuan Li,Shiyi Xiao,Pengyue Ma,Hanyan Cao,Feng Pan,Yuxuan Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出神经传输统一框架（NTU），通过代数结构对齐不同码距的译码任务，实现知识迁移加速大规模量子译码器训练。NTU-Transformer实例在平面表面码和二元自行车码上超越现有方法，为容错量子计算提供了可扩展的跨距离译码器训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础解码器在容错量子计算中表现出色，但随着码距增大，综合征生成和神经优化的成本急剧上升，存在严重的扩展瓶颈。

Method: 提出神经传输统一框架（NTU），利用可扩展码族共享的代数结构对齐不同码距间的译码任务，实现小码知识加速大规模解码器训练。具体实例化为NTU-Transformer，基于Transformer架构针对平面表面码和二元自行车码设计。

Result: 在平面表面码上，NTU-Transformer在电路级噪声下优于相关感知匹配算法，分别达到[361,1,19]码和[625,1,25]码的性能提升；在[72,12,6]二元自行车码上，在低物理错误率区域超越Relay-BP。

Conclusion: 该工作为容错量子处理器中基础解码器的跨距离摊销训练提供了可扩展的路线。

Abstract: Foundation decoders, a class of high-capacity neural decoders, are leading candidates for fault-tolerant quantum computing, with accurate and efficient decoding at large code distances. However, their construction often faces a steep scaling barrier, as larger code distances rapidly amplify the cost of syndrome generation and neural optimization. To address this bottleneck, here we devise neural transfer unification (NTU), a unified framework for efficient foundation decoders. A central feature of NTU is its ability to align decoding tasks across code distances via algebraic structures shared by scalable code families, which enables knowledge learned on smaller codes to accelerate large-scale decoder training. We instantiate NTU as NTU-Transformer, a transformer-based neural decoder tailored for planar surface codes and bivariate bicycle codes. For planar surface codes under circuit-level noise, NTU-Transformer outperforms correlation-aware matching on the $[\![361,1,19]\!]$ code and further scales to the $[\![625,1,25]\!]$ code, where it exceeds standard matching through transfer adaptation. For the bivariate bicycle code with $[\![72,12,6]\!]$, it surpasses Relay-BP in the low-physical-error regime. These results establish our proposal as a scalable route to amortized cross-distance training of foundation decoders for fault-tolerant quantum processors.

</details>


### [124] [Rate-2/3 Girth-8 (3,18)-Regular Quantum LDPC Codes from Two-Branch Finite-Field Bases and CPM Lifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27130)
*Koki Okada,Kenta Kasai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一款[[34542,23032,d≤310]]的rate-2/3量子LDPC码，采用(3,18)-正则双分支有限域基和度P=101的循环置换矩阵提升。该CSS码的Tanner图围长为8，在p=0.01下10^8次试验无译码失败，有限长FER转折点约在p=0.029。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有高错误纠错性能的有限长量子LDPC码，探索通过结构提升和译码实验获得紧的最小距离上界。

Method: 采用(3,18)-正则双分支有限域基和度P=101的循环置换矩阵(CPM)提升构造量子LDPC码；使用LLR联合置信传播(BP)结合确定性后处理进行译码实验。

Result: 得到参数[[34542,23032,d≤310]]的CSS码，Tanner图(H_X和H_Z)围长为8；在p=0.01误码率下10^8次试验无译码失败；有限长FER扫描估计转折点约在p=0.029。

Conclusion: 该构造实现了高码率(2/3)且译码性能优越的量子LDPC码，d≤310是通过结构提升和译码逻辑错误获得的最紧上界，并非真实最小距离的估计。

Abstract: We construct a rate-$2/3$ quantum low-density parity-check (LDPC) code from a $(3,18)$-regular two-branch finite-field base and a circulant-permutation-matrix (CPM) lift of degree $P=101$. The resulting code is a Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) code with parameters $[[34542,23032,d\le 310]]$. We do not regard this upper bound as an estimate of the true minimum distance; rather, $d\le310$ is the tightest upper bound currently obtained from structural lifts and decoder-produced logical errors. The construction has row weight 18 and column weight 3, and the Tanner graphs of $H_X$ and $H_Z$ separately have girth 8. Decoder experiments with log-likelihood-ratio (LLR) joint belief propagation (BP) and deterministic post-processing show no failures in $10^8$ trials at $p=0.01$, and a finite-length frame error rate (FER) sweep estimates the transition near $p=0.029$.

</details>


### [125] [Loss-aware pulse sequence optimization for generating photonic Fock states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27158)
*Benjamin Stodd,Priyanshu Tiwari,René Sondenheimer,Sina Saravi,Martin Gärttner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在混合腔系统中（非线性介质+二能级系统）制备频率可调的光子Fock态，通过梯度优化构建多脉冲驱动协议，实现近确定性低光子数Fock态制备，并在开放系统下识别出更具鲁棒性的脉冲序列，发现最优脉冲的相对相位严格限制为0或π。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现频率可调的光子Fock态的高效、确定性制备，并提高其在耗散环境下的鲁棒性。

Method: 采用基于梯度的优化方法构建多脉冲驱动协议，通过调控脉冲振幅、相位和脉冲间延迟来控制系统动力学；利用Lindblad主方程模拟原子衰变和光子损失，将优化扩展到开放系统；通过微扰分析对两脉冲序列进行解析研究。

Result: 在幺正动力学下实现了近确定性的低光子数Fock态制备；开放系统优化得到的脉冲序列比理想假设下优化的序列具有更强的抗耗散鲁棒性；最优脉冲序列的相对相位严格约束为0或π。

Conclusion: 该优化框架有效提升了Fock态制备的效率和鲁棒性，相位约束为实验设计提供了明确的指导原则。

Abstract: We investigate the preparation of frequency-tunable photonic Fock states in a hybrid cavity system consisting of a nonlinear medium and a two-level system. Employing a gradient-based optimization approach, we construct multipulse driving protocols that control the system dynamics through pulse amplitudes, phases, and inter-pulse delays. Assuming unitary dynamics, the optimized sequences enable near-deterministic preparation of low-photon-number Fock states. We extend the optimization framework to open-system dynamics by modeling atomic decay and photon loss within the Lindblad master equation. This allows us to identify pulse sequences that exhibit enhanced robustness against dissipation compared to those optimized under idealized assumptions. Furthermore, we find that optimal pulse sequences obey strict constraints on relative phases, which are limited to values of 0 or $π$. These phase restrictions are supported by an analytical study that investigates a simple two-pulse sequence treating the second pulse perturbatively.

</details>


### [126] [From Approximate Floquet Engineering to Full Floquet Theory: Coherent Control of Chiral Spin Systems in Spintronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27183)
*Andrea Simion,Claudiu Filip,Coriolan Tiusan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了完整的Floquet空间 formalism（源自NMR方法）来模拟存在静态磁场B0和横向振荡场B1时驱动耦合电子自旋的动力学，显式包含各向同性交换耦合J和手性Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用（DMI）。数值验证了Fourier空间截断的收敛性。结果表明：非相互作用极限下恢复标准驱动自旋动力学；单独交换耦合不改变集体自旋期望值；DMI产生Sy分量、抑制Sz分量并产生倾斜椭圆Bloch轨迹（开放边界条件显著，周期边界条件可忽略）；两者共存时动力学呈现多频扰动特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现对时间周期性驱动下相互作用自旋系统的相干控制是自旋量子技术的核心挑战。现有方法多限于简单相干旋转区域，缺乏能够统一处理交换耦合和DMI等相互作用的完整理论框架。

Method: 采用来自NMR的全Floquet空间形式，将驱动耦合电子自旋系统映射到Floquet空间中进行动力学建模，显式包含交换耦合J和DMI，并通过系统性的Fourier截断验证数值收敛性。

Result: 1) 非相互作用极限下恢复标准B1驱动的自旋振荡动力学；2) 单独交换耦合J不改变指定初态下的集体自旋期望值；3) DMI产生有限Sy、抑制Sz，产生倾斜椭圆Bloch球轨迹；4) 开放边界下DMI效应显著，周期边界下几乎可忽略；5) J与DMI共存时产生强扰动多频动力学。

Conclusion: Floquet空间形式提供了一种鲁棒且可预测的框架，用于分析和工程化驱动相互作用自旋系统的相干动力学，超越了简单相干旋转区域。

Abstract: Coherent control of interacting spin systems under time-periodic driving is a central challenge in spin-based quantum technologies. Here we demonstrate the applicability of a full Floquet-space formalism, adapted from Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR) methodologies, to model the dynamics of driven coupled electron spins in the presence of a static magnetic field B0 and a transverse oscillating field B1. The framework explicitly includes isotropic exchange coupling J and the chiral Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya antisymmetric exchange interaction (DMI), and its numerical convergence is systematically validated with respect to Fourier-space truncation. In the non-interacting limit, the expected driven-spin dynamics is recovered, with the oscillation periodicity governed by B1. Exchange coupling alone does not modify the collective spin expectation values under the chosen initial condition, consistent with symmetry considerations. In contrast, increasing DMI generates a finite expectation value of Sy, suppresses the expectation value of Sz, and produces tilted, elliptical Bloch-sphere trajectories, reflecting the emergence of chiral spin-spin correlations. These effects are pronounced for open boundary conditions, while remaining nearly negligible in the periodic boundary case. When exchange coupling and DMI coexist, the dynamics becomes strongly perturbed and multi-frequency in nature. Together, these results demonstrate that full Floquet-space modeling provides a robust and predictive framework for analyzing and engineering coherent dynamics in driven interacting spin systems beyond simple coherent-rotation regimes.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum group codes for non-Clifford logic: enhanced decoding, addressability and parallelizability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27211)
*Jean Gasnier,Virgile Guémard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于经典准群码的量子群码框架，支持可寻址且可并行化的横向多控制-Z门，并通过类域论提升过程构造了具有改进解码复杂度和逻辑门并行性的好量子群码，解码时间从三次降至准二次，使魔法态蒸馏协议的时间复杂度降低近线性因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子AG码解码复杂度高（三次时间），且缺乏支持可寻址可并行化非Clifford门的量子码构造。本文旨在通过类域论提升经典AG码构造量子群码，降低解码复杂度同时保持横向门的可寻址性和并行性。

Method: 基于经典准群码定义量子群码（量子CSS码的子类）；利用类域论中的提升过程将好量子AG码的底层经典AG码提升为量子群码，支持横向C^m Z门及可寻址可并行化的C^{m-1}Z门；解码采用准二次时间算法。

Result: 构造的量子群码支持横向C^m Z门和可寻址可并行化的C^{m-1}Z门；解码器具有准二次时间复杂度和线性解码半径，优于已有量子AG码的三次时间复杂度。

Conclusion: 本工作使魔法态蒸馏协议的时间复杂度降低近线性因子，为容错量子计算中非Clifford门的实现提供了更高效的编码方案。

Abstract: We introduce a framework based on classical quasi group codes to define a class of quantum CSS codes, called quantum group codes, supporting transversal multi-control-$Z$ gates which are both addressable and parallelizable, thus allowing to efficiently implement circuits composed of non-Clifford gates at the logical level. Building on this, we use a lifting procedure of classical AG codes established from class field theory to construct good quantum group codes with improved decoding complexity and logical multi-control-$Z$ gate parallelizability. More precisely, on input a good quantum AG code over the alphabet $\mathbb F_q$ with transversal $\mathsf{C}^m\mathsf Z$ gate, we apply this lifting procedure to its underlying classical AG code and obtain a quantum group code over the alphabet $\mathbb F_{q^2}$ supporting a transversal $\mathsf{C}^m\mathsf Z$ gate as well as addressable and parallelizable $\mathsf{C}^{m-1}\mathsf Z$ gates. In addition, this quantum code admits a quasi-quadratic time decoder with a linear decoding radius. This is to be compared with the previous quantum AG codes which have a cubic-time decoder. Hence, our work implies a decrease of the time complexity of state-of-the-art magic-state distillation protocols by an almost linear factor.

</details>


### [128] [A 0.651-approximation to quantum Max Cut via Rydberg atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27224)
*Tomás Crosta,Matthieu Saubanere,Felix Huber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子Max Cut问题（反铁磁海森堡哈密顿量），提出了一种结合里德伯原子系统的量子动力学、半定规划和随机取样的混合近似算法，实现了0.651的条件近似比，优于纯半定规划的0.614，且对退火过程不完美具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Max Cut是QMA完全问题，作为量子物理中近似算法的基准。现有最优方法仅依赖半定规划，近似比为0.614，存在提升空间。

Method: 混合方法：利用里德伯原子系统的自然量子动力学执行量子退火，结合半定规划进行经典优化，最后通过随机取样得到近似解。

Result: 条件近似比达到0.651，优于纯半定规划的0.614；即使里德伯退火只能获得真实基态能量的89%，优势仍然保持。

Conclusion: 开创了量子-经典混合优化算法的新路径，展示了量子系统与经典优化方法结合的有效性。

Abstract: Quantum Max Cut, also known as the anti-ferromagnetic Heisenberg Hamiltonian, is a QMA-complete problem which serves as a benchmark for approximation algorithms in quantum physics. Here we develop a hybrid approximation algorithm to quantum Max Cut, which uses the natural quantum dynamics of Rydberg atom systems in combination with semidefinite programming and randomized rounding. It achieves a conditional approximation ratio of $0.651$, compared to the best-known ratio of $0.614$ that relies on semidefinite programming alone. The algorithm is robust in the sense that the advantage persists even if the annealing procedure of the Rydberg atom system obtains a state whose energy is only $89\%$ of its true ground state energy. Our approach opens a new route for hybrid quantum-classical algorithms that combine quantum with classical optimization methods.

</details>


### [129] [A hardware-safety-gated system for LLM-written native ARTIQ control code on a trapped-ion platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27231)
*Duanyang Wang,Lu Qi,Yuanheng Xie,Norbert M. Linke,Kenneth R. Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带安全边界的LLM智能体控制系统，用于囚禁离子实验。该系统通过MCP服务器连接ARTIQ控制栈，要求所有硬件操作指令必须携带授权令牌。令牌通过硬件仿真自动审核或人工授权两种方式发放，确保智能体在不破坏设备的前提下自主开发实验。在40Ca+/40CaOH+晶体上成功实现了自动校准栈构建和磁场稳定闭环，并在171Yb+平台上验证了跨平台可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在自主控制实验设备时，未经检查的代码可能损坏实验设备，且缺乏形式化的操作级边界来区分人类授权/监督与智能体决策。现有工作未解决这一安全问题。

Method: 设计了一个三层安全控制系统：1) LLM智能体通过MCP服务器与ARTIQ栈交互；2) 所有工具调用必须携带内容绑定的授权令牌才能操作硬件；3) 令牌通过两种方式发放——自动方式（在dax.sim硬件模拟器上运行脚本并逐操作检查预设备界限）或手动方式（人类操作员针对敏感操作授权）。智能体在此边界内自主开发实验而非仅调用预制程序。

Result: 在40Ca+/40CaOH+共囚禁晶体上，智能体自主构建了完整校准栈，并在操作员指导下闭环了跨仪器磁场稳定系统。在独立的171Yb+平台上验证了接口级可移植性。通过对抗性脚本系统测试了令牌授权机制的安全边界。分析了智能体仍需人类指导的环节，发现其局限在于元认知控制（识别何时需要重新定义问题）而非领域知识。

Conclusion: 提出的安全边界系统能有效保护实验设备免受LLM智能体失控代码的损害，同时保留智能体自主开发实验的能力。智能体的主要限制在于元认知层面的问题重构能力而非专业知识，这为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) agents can write and run experimental control code. This allows laboratory work to be conducted autonomously. However, this autonomy raises a safety problem that prior work has not addressed. Unchecked code can damage the apparatus, and there is no formal, per-operation boundary between human authorization/supervision, and agent decisions. We present a control system that places an LLM agent in the loop of a trapped-ion experiment while enforcing such a boundary. The agent controls the existing Advanced Real-Time Infrastructure for Quantum physics (ARTIQ) stack through tools provided by a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server. No tool call reaches the hardware unless it carries an authorization token bound to its exact contents. Tokens are issued in one of two ways: automatically, by running the agent's proposed script in an isolated hardware simulation (dax.sim) and checking every operation against preset per-device bounds, or manually by a human operator for sensitive actions. Within this boundary the agent develops its own experiments, rather than only calling pre-built routines. We deploy the system on a co-trapped $^{40}$Ca$^{+}$/$^{40}$CaOH$^{+}$ crystal, where the agent autonomously builds a full calibration stack and, with targeted operator guidance, closes a cross-instrument magnetic-field-stabilization loop. On a separate, independent $^{171}$Yb$^{+}$ platform, we confirm interface-level portability. We systematically test token-authorization mechanism with adversarial scripts that attempt to bypass it, mapping the precise boundary of its protection and prioritizing where to strengthen it next. Analyzing where the agent still requires human guidance, we find that its limits lie in metacognitive control, namely recognizing when a problem must be re-framed, rather than in domain knowledge.

</details>


### [130] [Quantum computer architecture with ions in tweezer arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27249)
*Benjamin F. Schiffer,Christopher Monroe,Peter Zoller,J. Ignacio Cirac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于光镊阵列捕获离子的量子计算机架构，利用有效电偶极子间的库仑相互作用实现纠缠门，并展示了可扩展离子-光镊量子处理器的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结合 trapped-ion 的长相干时间和光镊平台的可重构性与并行操作能力，克服现有离子阱量子计算架构在可扩展性方面的局限。

Method: 将选定离子输运至局部相互作用区，激发至辅助态以产生可控有效电偶极子，利用库仑相互作用介导纠缠门机制，实现质心与相对运动轨迹的温度鲁棒闭合。

Result: 所提出的纠缠门机制能够精确、温度鲁棒地闭合质心和相对运动轨迹，消除量子比特与运动之间的残余纠缠；基于钡离子的具体实现展示了态选择极化率及并行门执行中的串扰抑制效果。

Conclusion: 该工作为实现可扩展的离子-光镊量子处理器提供了现实可行的技术路线。

Abstract: We propose a quantum computer architecture based on ions confined in optical tweezer arrays, combining the long coherence times of trapped-ion qubits with the reconfigurability and parallel operation enabled by tweezer platforms. Selected ions are transported to local interaction zones, where excitation to an auxiliary state with a displaced optical potential generates a controllable effective electric dipole. We develop and analyze entangling-gate mechanisms mediated by the Coulomb interaction between such effective dipoles, and show that they enable precise, temperature-robust closure of the center-of-mass and relative motional trajectories, leaving no residual entanglement between the qubits and the motion. We further outline a concrete implementation with barium ions based on state-selective polarizability, and study the suppression of cross-talk during parallel gate execution, with relevance to transversal gates in quantum error correction. Our results thereby establish a realistic route toward scalable ion-tweezer quantum processors.

</details>


### [131] [Particle-preserving fermionic shadows with mode-independent sample complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27254)
*Maxwell West,M. Cerezo,Martin Larocca*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过经典影子方法学习费米子态中粒子数守恒算符期望值的问题，主要贡献包括：将Slater行列式态重叠的最坏情况样本复杂度从$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt n \log n)$改进到$\mathcal{O}(\eta\log\eta)$；对一般二次费米子可观测量给出了$\mathcal{O}(\eta\|h_0\|_2^2)$的样本复杂度；并讨论了高效量子电路实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在平均情况下能以常数样本数学习Slater行列式态的重叠，但最坏情况下的样本复杂度界限为$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt n \log n)$，存在改进空间；同时希望建立粒子数守恒费米子算符期望值学习的通用理论框架。

Method: 将问题约化为AIII对称空间$U(n)/(U(\eta)\times U(n-\eta))$上的调和分析，利用Jacobi系综和正交多项式理论计算极值影子方差；经典后处理采用$\mathcal{O}(n\eta^2)$和$\mathcal{O}(n^2\eta)$的算法；量子实现采用第一量子化编码下的近似酉设计，电路深度为模式数的多对数级别。

Result: Slater行列式态重叠的最坏情况样本复杂度改进为$\mathcal{O}(\eta\log\eta)$（与模式数$n$无关）；二次费米子可观测量$h$的样本复杂度为$\mathcal{O}(\eta\|h_0\|_2^2)$；经典后处理复杂度分别为$\mathcal{O}(n\eta^2)$和$\mathcal{O}(n^2\eta)$；量子电路深度为多对数级别。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上显著改进了费米子态学习的最坏情况样本复杂度，建立了与模式数无关的界限，同时证明了计算有效性和量子实现的可行性。所发展的调和分析与正交多项式方法具有独立理论价值。

Abstract: We consider the problem of learning expectation values of particle-preserving operators with respect to an unknown $η$-particle $n$-mode fermionic state via classical shadows. Our main application is to estimating overlaps with arbitrary Slater determinant states: While it is known that such overlaps can, in the average case, be learnt to a fixed additive precision with a constant number of samples, the best-known worst case bound is $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt n \log n)$; here we improve this to $\mathcal{O}(η\logη)$, achieving a mode-independent sample cost. Our procedure is also computationally efficient, requiring only classical post-processing which for a generic dense orbital runs in time $\mathcal{O}(nη^2)$. For the task of estimating the expectation value of a general particle-preserving quadratic fermionic observable $h$, we prove a sample complexity bound of $\mathcal{O}(η\|h_0\|_2^2)$, where $h_0$ is the traceless component of $h$; the associated classical post-processing scales as $\mathcal{O}(n^2η)$. Finally, we discuss implementation of the required randomization: in a first-quantized encoding, approximate unitary designs give circuit depths polylogarithmic in the number of modes, contrasting with linear-depth requirements for nearest-neighbor second-quantized matchgate implementations. On the technical side, our proof reduces the extremal shadow variance to harmonic analysis on the AIII symmetric space $U(n)/(U(η)\times U(n-η))$ and evaluates the resulting integral using techniques from the theories of Jacobi ensembles and orthogonal polynomials, in a calculation which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [132] [Large-scale multimode entangling-gate synthesis in trapped-ion systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27266)
*YingYe Huang,Wentao Chen,Guoyu Zou,Xuan Fan,Jing-Ning Zhang,Kihwan Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多音调门合成的数值框架，通过交替最小化策略直接搜索满足竞争性要求的控制场。在离子链规模达N=1000时仍能合成全连接和最近邻相互作用门，仅使用全局激光控制。控制资源随系统规模增长缓慢，并扩展到N=512的qLDPC目标。该工作为大规模可编程相互作用工程提供了可行路线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着离子阱中离子数量增加，集体运动模式密度增大，导致多模纠缠门合成变得极具挑战性。设计大规模门需要同时实现目标自旋-自旋相互作用、抑制残余自旋-运动纠缠、限制实验控制资源，这是一个高维非凸优化问题。

Method: 开发了一个多音调门合成的数值框架，采用交替最小化策略直接搜索满足竞争性要求的控制场。该框架提高了数值稳定性，适用于具有大量运动模式和目标相互作用的大系统。

Result: 在N=1000的离子链上成功合成了全连接和最近邻相互作用门，仅使用全局激光控制。控制资源随系统规模的增长速度缓慢。将框架扩展到N=512的结构化qLDPC目标。

Conclusion: 多模门合成是实现大规模可编程相互作用工程的一种可行途径，为可编程离子阱量子处理器中的相互作用工程设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Trapped-ion systems have emerged as a leading platform for scalable quantum information processing owing to their high-fidelity operations and long-range entangling capabilities. As the number of ions in a trap increases, the growing density of collective motional modes makes the synthesis of multimode entangling gates increasingly challenging. Designing large-scale gates requires simultaneously realizing the desired spin-spin interactions, suppressing residual spin-motion entanglement, and limiting experimental control resources, leading to a high-dimensional non-convex optimization problem. Here we develop a numerical framework for multi-tone gate synthesis that directly searches for control fields satisfying these competing requirements. By employing an alternating-minimization strategy, the framework improves numerical stability and remains effective for large systems with many motional modes and target interactions. As representative demonstrations, we synthesize gates implementing all-to-all and nearest-neighbor interaction patterns in ion chains of up to N = 1000, using only global laser control. Across the parameter regimes explored here, the control resources required to maintain high-fidelity interactions do not exhibit rapid growth with system size. We extend the framework to individual addressing using a structured qLDPC target at N = 512 as an example. These results identify multimode gate synthesis as a viable route toward programmable interaction engineering in large-scale trapped-ion quantum processors.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum-Limited Subdiffraction Telescopy Requires Genuine Multi-Telescope Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27276)
*Yujie Zhang,Yunkai Wang,Wilson Wu,Thomas Jennewein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 传统恒星干涉测量仅依赖望远镜对之间的相干性，对于量子极限下的亚衍射成像，这种成对测量不足以估计高阶图像矩（2阶以上）。论文证明在N望远镜阵列中，任意成对测量只能达到全阵列量子费舍信息（QFI）标度至第2阶，而估计更高阶矩需要真正的多望远镜干涉。作者提出了阵列SPADE测量方案，可达到最优QFI标度，并可在量子网络架构中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究传统恒星干涉测量中仅基于望远镜对之间相互相干性的测量是否足以在量子极限下实现亚衍射成像，以及能否估计高阶图像矩。

Method: 理论推导N望远镜阵列下图像矩的量子费舍信息标度，证明成对测量的局限性；构造基于空间模式解复用（SPADE）的阵列测量方案；提出在量子网络架构中嵌入这些测量的方法。

Result: 任意成对测量只能达到全阵列QFI标度至第2阶，更高阶矩需要多望远镜干涉；阵列SPADE测量可达到最优QFI标度直至有限阵列截断。

Conclusion: 实现量子极限下高阶图像矩估计必须依赖多望远镜干涉，而阵列SPADE测量结合量子网络架构为长基线望远镜提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conventional stellar interferometry reconstructs incoherent sources from pairwise mutual coherences between telescopes. Are such pairwise measurements sufficient for quantum-limited subdiffraction imaging with a telescope array? We show that for generic image-moment estimation, they are not. We consider weak incoherent light from a generic extended source observed by an array of telescopes, each supporting a single optical mode. For an N-telescope array, we derive the quantum Fisher information (QFI) scaling of image moments up to the cutoff 2N-2 and prove that arbitrary measurements restricted to telescope pairs attain the full-array QFI scaling only up to second order. Thus, estimating higher-order moments at the quantum limit requires genuinely multi-telescope interference. Inspired by spatial-mode demultiplexing (SPADE) from single-aperture subdiffraction imaging, we construct array-SPADE measurements that attain the optimal QFI scaling up to the finite-array cutoff. Finally, we show that these measurements can, in principle, be embedded in ancilla- and memory-assisted quantum-network architectures for long-baseline telescopy.

</details>


### [134] [Exploring dynamics of individual vortices in a superconductor via a levitated magnetic transducer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27297)
*Yiqi Wang,Trisha Madhavan,J. DaLi Schaefer,Addison NewRingeisen,Frankie Fung,Mikhail D. Lukin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用微米级悬浮磁颗粒的机械运动探测YBCO超导薄膜中单个涡旋的动力学行为，观察到机械频率、耗散率和能量的随机电报信号，揭示了涡旋隧穿和非指数衰减的涡旋-缺陷相互作用动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导体中俘获涡旋决定了其基本性质并在磁悬浮等应用中至关重要，但其复杂动力学仍知之甚少，因此需要新的探测方法以理解单个涡旋的行为。

Method: 利用微米级悬浮磁颗粒的机械运动作为探针，通过观测悬浮颗粒的机械频率、耗散率和能量变化，研究YBCO超导薄膜中俘获的单个涡旋动力学。

Result: 观测到机械频率、耗散率和能量的随机电报信号（归因于单个涡旋的随机隧穿）；涡旋-缺陷相互作用的非线性导致环降测量中的非指数衰减行为。

Conclusion: 揭示了超导悬浮系统中难以捉摸的耗散机制，开辟了利用悬浮磁体作为敏感探针研究超导体中单个涡旋静态和动态性质的新途径，并为实现高相干机械换能器提供了新思路。

Abstract: Trapped vortices determine fundamental properties of superconductors and play an important role in many practical applications such as magnetic levitation, however their complex dynamics remain poorly understood. Here, we use the mechanical motion of micron-scale levitated magnetic particles to probe the dynamics of individual vortices. Specifically, we show that the dynamics of levitated magnets are strongly influenced by vortices trapped in the YBCO superconducting film. We observe random telegraph signals in the mechanical frequency, dissipation rate, and energy of levitated particles, which we attribute to random tunneling of individual vortices. The nonlinearity of vortex-defect interaction manifests as non-exponential decay in ringdown measurements, revealing a complex underlying potential landscape. Our results provide insights into elusive dissipation mechanisms in superconducting levitated systems, open new avenues for using levitated magnets as sensitive probes of static and dynamic properties of individual vortices in superconductors and their interactions with material disorder, and point toward novel routes for using magnetic particles as highly coherent mechanical transducers.

</details>


### [135] [Self-Sifting quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27299)
*Saman Sarshar,Mostafa Annabestani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型双向量子密钥分发(QKD)协议，利用最大纠缠Bell态的一个量子比特作为量子信道，并引入基于扰乱算子的新安全机制。所有筛选和窃听检测操作都在量子通信阶段完成后由Bob单独执行，控制模式永不公开宣布，从而有效防止模式依赖攻击。飞行量子比特不直接编码密钥信息，限制了从量子信道攻击中提取的信息量，且本应丢弃的轮次可用于检测窃听者。分析表明，最一般形式的基于辅助系统的攻击是可检测的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双向QKD协议中，筛选操作和窃听检测需要公开宣布控制模式，这使得攻击者能够根据模式信息实施模式依赖攻击或隐藏在控制模式中不被发现。此外，传统协议中传输的量子比特直接编码密钥信息，导致一旦量子信道被攻击，密钥信息可能泄露。本研究旨在设计一种更安全的双向QKD协议，克服上述安全漏洞。

Method: 协议使用最大纠缠Bell态的一个量子比特作为量子信道进行双向密钥分发。核心机制包括：(1) 引入扰乱算子作为安全机制；(2) 所有筛选和窃听检测操作推迟到量子通信阶段完成后，且仅由Bob执行；(3) 控制模式永不公开宣布；(4) 飞行量子比特不直接编码密钥信息；(5) 本应丢弃的轮次被重新用于窃听检测。安全性分析针对最广泛的一类基于辅助系统的攻击(ancilla-based attacks)进行。

Result: 理论分析表明，该协议能够有效抵御最一般形式的基于辅助系统的攻击。由于控制模式永不公开，任何依赖模式信息的攻击策略都被固有地阻止。飞行量子比特不直接携带密钥信息，显著限制了攻击者从量子信道攻击中获取的信息量。原本被丢弃的轮次转化为额外的窃听检测资源，增强了检测能力。

Conclusion: 该双向QKD协议通过创新的安全架构（控制模式隐藏、密钥信息不直接编码、废弃轮次重利用）在安全性方面优于传统方案，能够检测最一般形式的基于辅助系统的攻击，为量子密钥分发提供了更强的安全保障。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel two-way quantum key distribution (QKD) protocol in which the sender (Alice) and receiver (Bob) employ one qubit of a maximally entangled Bell state as the quantum channel for key exchange. The protocol incorporates a new security mechanism based on a scrambling operator. Unlike conventional two-way QKD protocols, all sifting operations and eavesdropper detection procedures are postponed until the completion of the quantum communication stage and are performed exclusively by Bob. Since the control mode is never publicly announced, attacks that rely on mode-dependent adaptations or attempt to remain hidden within the control mode are inherently prevented. Furthermore, the traveling qubit does not directly encode key information, substantially limiting the information that can be extracted from attacks targeting the quantum channel alone. An additional distinctive feature of the protocol is that rounds that would ordinarily be discarded can instead be utilized to detect the presence of an eavesdropper. We analyze a broad class of ancilla-based attacks, in which an eavesdropper couples an ancillary system to the transmitted qubit in an attempt to gain information about the key, and show that such attacks are detectable in their most general form.

</details>


### [136] [Cultivating logical catalysts for fault-tolerant dyadic phase rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27358)
*Yichen Xu,Xiao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种表面码培育协议，用于创建可重复使用的逻辑催化剂态，通过相位反冲精确实现细粒度二进相位门 Z^{2^{-b}}。该催化剂是高周期Clifford电路U的本征态，直接构建在O(2^b)个逻辑量子比特上。每次调用通过受控-U gadget实现目标相位，消除了Clifford+T合成近似误差，使在线非Clifford深度与目标逻辑精度无关。以sqrt(T)=Z^{1/8}为例，U为9量子比特砖墙Clifford电路，受控-U包含8个受控CNOT。从9个距离3旋转表面码开始，通过逻辑-U校验、纠错提取和后选择、码生长及互补间隙解码培育催化剂。由于相位读出的固有容错性，单轮验证即可达到领先的纠错缩放。在物理错误率p=10^{-3}下，后选择催化剂可生长至距离7旋转表面码，逻辑泄漏率约10^{-6}，平均约7次尝试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错量子计算中，实现非Clifford门（如T门）通常依赖魔法态蒸馏或Clifford+T合成近似，这些方法存在近似误差、高开销或在线深度随精度增长的问题。本文旨在通过可重复使用的逻辑催化剂态，实现精确、低开销、恒定深度的细粒度二进相位门，避免近似误差并降低在线非Clifford深度。

Method: 提出表面码培育协议，构建高周期Clifford电路U的本征态作为催化剂，通过受控-U gadget实现相位反冲。采用逻辑-U校验、纠错提取、后选择、码生长和互补间隙解码等容错技术。具体以Z^{1/8}为例，设计9量子比特砖墙Clifford电路，使用8个受控CNOT实现受控-U。通过混合张量网络和稳定子模拟验证协议性能。

Result: 单轮验证即可达到领先的纠错缩放性能。在物理错误率p=10^{-3}下，后选择催化剂可生长至距离7旋转表面码，逻辑泄漏率约10^{-6}，平均约7次尝试即可成功。通过更强的后选择可进一步抑制泄漏率。

Conclusion: 该协议用离线的、特定相位的催化剂培育换取了在线实现的精确性、可重用性和恒定深度。与现有方案相比，在具有受限横向门集的编码中，该方法为固定细粒度二进相位门提供了更优的实现方案。

Abstract: We introduce a surface-code cultivation protocol for reusable logical catalyst states that implement exact fine dyadic phase gates $Z^{2^{-b}}$ by phase kickback. The catalyst is an eigenstate of a high-period Clifford circuit $U$, with a direct construction supported on $O(2^b)$ logical qubits. Once cultivated, each invocation implements the target phase through a controlled-$U$ gadget, removing Clifford+$T$ synthesis approximation error from the online gate and making the online non-Clifford depth independent of the target logical accuracy. As a concrete demonstration, we construct a catalyst for $\sqrt{T}=Z^{1/8}$, where $U$ is a nine-qubit brickwork Clifford circuit and controlled-$U$ consists of eight controlled-CNOTs. Starting from nine distance-three rotated-surface-code blocks, we cultivate the catalyst through logical-$U$ checks, syndrome extraction and postselection, code growth, and complementary-gap decoding. Due to the intrinsic fault tolerance of the phase read-out, a \emph{single} verification round already reaches the leading error-corrected scaling, in contrast to the repeated logical checks required when cultivating single-qubit magic states. A hybrid tensor-network and stabilizer simulation shows that, at physical error rate $p=10^{-3}$, the postselected catalyst can be grown to distance-seven rotated-surface-code blocks with logical leakage rate $\sim 10^{-6}$ using around seven expected attempts, and can be suppressed further with stronger postselection. Compared with existing protocols, our approach trades offline, phase-specific catalyst cultivation for exactness, reusability, and constant-depth online implementation of fixed fine dyadic phases in codes with restricted transversal gate sets.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

### [137] [pyDOF: a Python library for the design of discrete forward and inverse filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26830)
*Z. Nikolaou,P. Domingo,L. Vervisch,D. Drikakis*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: pyDOF是一个基于Python的软件库，用于设计对称、物理空间的离散正向和逆向滤波器，基于约束优化框架，支持多种滤波器类型和约束条件，主要面向计算流体动力学但也可用于通用信号处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种灵活、通用的离散滤波器设计工具，使用户能够通过约束优化框架自定义滤波器传递函数，满足计算流体动力学及其他信号处理应用的需求。

Method: 基于先前工作中开发的约束优化框架，支持单调性、正定性、值固定、梯度平滑等多种约束；包含自适应滤波器模板选择、van Cittert逆向滤波器设计（用户可控重建阶数）；滤波器系数自动计算并保存为纯文本文件。

Result: 实现了低通、高通、多带通/带阻等多种离散滤波器的自动设计，支持用户自定义目标传递函数，滤波器系数可直接被任何编程语言解析使用。

Conclusion: pyDOF作为一个通用且抽象的滤波器设计框架，虽然主要为计算流体动力学开发，但可广泛应用于各类信号处理场景。

Abstract: In this work, we present pyDOF, a Python-based software library which provides a domain-specific framework for the design of symmetric, physical-space, forward as well as inverse discrete filters. pyDOF is based on a constrained optimisation framework developed in our previous work [1, 2]. This framework allows the user to impose a wide range of constraints on the discrete filter transfer-function such as monotonicity, positivity, value-fixing, gradient-smoothing etc. amongst many others. pyDOF additionally includes an adaptive filter stencil selection option, and a van Cittert-based inverse-filter design with a user-controlled reconstruction order. The filter coefficients are computed automatically, and saved to a plain text file which can be readily parsed by any programming language. pyDOF can be used to design a wide range of low-pass, high-pass, multi band-pass/band-stop etc. discrete filters. In addition, due to its generality and abstraction, pyDOF can be used to design specific filters for user-defined target filter transfer functions. Although developed primarily for application to computational fluid dynamics simulations, pyDOF can be used to design discrete filters for a wide range of signal processing applications.

</details>


### [138] [GPU-accelerated superiorization on constrained physical problems with SupPy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27086)
*Tobias Becher,Yair Censor,Kay Barshad,Niklas Wahl*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SupPy——一个开源的Python工具箱，用于实现超优化方法（SM），并将其应用于三个物理问题：地震图像重建、低剂量CT重建和调强放疗治疗计划。实验表明，超优化算法在降低噪声、减少辐射剂量等方面优于仅寻求可行解的算法，并且在放疗中能在不可行约束集上产生临床可行的计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超优化方法（SM）在寻求可行解和约束优化之间提供了一个实用折中，但缺乏开源软件限制了其广泛应用。本文旨在通过开发SupPy工具箱并验证其在物理问题中的有效性，推动该方法的应用。

Method: 开发了SupPy——一个模块化的开源Python工具箱，支持在CPU和GPU上执行寻求可行解算法及其超优化版本。将超优化方法应用于三个物理问题：地震图像重建、低剂量CT重建和调强放疗治疗计划。

Result: 在三个应用场景中，超优化算法均优于仅寻求可行解的方法：成像案例中噪声降低，放疗计划中身体剂量降低。放疗案例中，超优化还能在不可行约束集上产生临床可行的计划。

Conclusion: 超优化方法在多个物理应用中具有实用价值，SupPy工具箱的发布将降低研究人员探索该方法的使用门槛，促进其更广泛的应用。

Abstract: The superiorization method (SM) is situated between feasibility-seeking and constrained optimization. Instead of aiming at the minimum of a given objective function over a constraint set, it seeks a feasible point at which the objective function value is reduced - though not necessarily minimal - rather than hard targets, or in which a mathematically optimal solution is not strictly required. While the method has been investigated for several applications in physics, its broader use has been limited, in part due to the lack of openly available software for researchers wishing to explore it.
  In this work we apply superiorization to three problems from applied physics: seismic image reconstruction, low-dose CT reconstruction and intensity-modulated radiotherapy treatment planning. These experiments are conducted with SupPy, an open-source modularized Python toolbox developed for this work, which supports execution of feasibility-seeking algorithms and their superiorized version on both the CPU and the GPU. In all three cases the superiorized algorithms achieve favorable results compared to feasibility-seeking alone, with reduced noise in the imaging examples and lowered body dose in the radiotherapy plans. For the radiotherapy case we further observe that superiorization produces clinically viable plans on infeasible constraint sets.

</details>


### [139] [Real Quantum Chemistry With Complex Orbitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27160)
*Hugo Åström,Susi Lehtola*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种在现有量子化学实数基组框架中实现复数基函数和复数哈密顿量的方法，用于计算线性分子在平行磁场中的性质。通过额外幺正变换实现复数计算，证明了复数基组在强磁场（0.6 B₀）下对正确对称性态的描述是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期研究[Åström and Lehtola, 2023]使用了近似的实值模型研究原子在磁场中的基组截断误差，但未能处理真正的复数哈密顿量。本文旨在实现一套方案，允许在常规实数基组量子化学软件中引入复数基函数和真实的复数哈密顿量，从而正确处理磁场下的电子结构问题。

Method: 在传统Fock构建前后执行额外的幺正变换，使现有软件方法和算法得以复用。采用未收缩的aug-cc-pVTZ和基准级AHGBSP3-9高斯型轨道基组，对Z≤18原子在静态磁场（最高0.6 B₀）的低能构型进行计算，比较实数与复数轨道计算的全数值完备基组极限结果。

Result: 实数哈密顿量的态是真实本征态的叠加，复数计算能正确捕获这些态。复数基组机制对于在研究的磁场强度范围内以正确对称性瞄准目标态是必要的。该方法为未来优化有限场计算的基组铺平了道路。

Conclusion: 复数基组是强磁场下量子化学计算的必要工具，本研究提供的关键技术可在现有实数基组软件框架中实现复数计算，为后续基组优化工作奠定基础。

Abstract: We follow up our study of basis set truncation errors for atoms in magnetic fields [Åström and Lehtola, J. Phys. Chem. A, 2023, 127, 10872]. Our previous study employed an approximate real-valued model. In this work, we implement a scheme to allow the use of complex basis functions and the true, complex Hamiltonian with linear molecules in a parallel magnetic field within the usual real-basis machinery of quantum chemistry. Our method performs additional unitary transformations before and after a conventional Fock build, thus allowing the reuse of existing software methods and algorithms. We apply our approach to calculations on low-lying configurations of the atoms $Z \leq 18$ in static magnetic fields up to 0.6 $B_0$. The calculations employ the uncontracted aug-cc-pVTZ and the benchmarking quality AHGBSP3-9 Gaussian-type orbital basis sets. We compare total energies obtained with real and complex orbitals using these basis sets to fully numerical ones at the complete basis set limit. We see that the states of the real-valued Hamiltonian are superpositions of the true eigenstates that are correctly captured by the complex calculations. Our results show that the complex basis machinery is necessary for targeting states with the correct symmetry for the studied range of magnetic field strengths. The novel tool is key for future work where we aim to optimize basis sets for finite-field calculations.

</details>
